`client/dllmain.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "process_watcher.h"
#include "file_integrity_check.h"
#include "network_reporter.h"
#include "self_integrity_guard.h"
#include "secret_obf.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <thread>
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <objidl.h>
#include <gdiplus.h>
#pragma comment(lib, "gdiplus.lib")

// Global instance dari ProcessWatcher
static Oblivion::ProcessWatcher* g_pProcessWatcher = nullptr;

// Buat instance global NetworkReporter agar bisa diakses
// Ganti 127.0.0.1 dengan IP server Anda
static Oblivion::NetworkReporter* g_pNetworkReporter = nullptr;
static Oblivion::SelfIntegrityGuard* g_pSelfIntegrityGuard = nullptr;
static HMODULE g_hSelfModule = nullptr;

// Fungsi export dari Stud_PE
// Ini adalah fungsi yang akan dipanggil oleh client setelah DLL di-inject.
// Untuk saat ini kita biarkan kosong karena inisialisasi utama ada di DllMain.
extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) void Oblivion_Entry() {
    // Potensial untuk trigger manual jika diperlukan di masa depan
}

// Fungsi helper untuk melaporkan pelanggaran
void ReportViolationAndExit(const std::wstring& message, const std::string& type, const std::string& details) {
    // Tunggu maksimal 5 detik untuk NetworkReporter siap (jika belum siap)
    int waitCount = 0;
    while (!g_pNetworkReporter && waitCount < 50) {
        Sleep(100); // Tunggu 100ms
        waitCount++;
    }
    
    // Kirim laporan ke server (BLOCKING - menunggu respons)
    // ReportViolation akan menunggu respons dari server dan menampilkan message box
    // Jika server mengirim KICK, ReportViolation akan memanggil ExitProcess
    if (g_pNetworkReporter) {
        g_pNetworkReporter->ReportViolation(type, details);
        // Jika sampai di sini, berarti server tidak mengirim KICK atau timeout
        // Tampilkan pesan default dan tutup client
        if (!message.empty()) {
            MessageBoxW(NULL, message.c_str(), L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Violation", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
        }
        ExitProcess(0);
    } else {
        // Jika reporter belum siap setelah timeout, fallback ke penutupan lokal
        if (!message.empty()) {
            MessageBoxW(NULL, message.c_str(), L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Violation", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
        }
        ExitProcess(0);
    }
}

// buat disable atau enable debug console
bool ShouldHideDebugConsole() {
    constexpr int kConsoleToggle = 1; // 0 = console tampil, 1 = console tersembunyi
    return kConsoleToggle == 1;
}

// Fungsi inisialisasi yang dijalankan di thread terpisah
void InitializeSecuritySystem() {
    // Tampilkan Splash Screen (non-blocking terhadap loader, tapi menjalankan loop singkat)
    auto ShowSplash = []() {
        using namespace Gdiplus;
        ULONG_PTR gdiplusToken = 0;
        GdiplusStartupInput gdiplusStartupInput;
        if (GdiplusStartup(&gdiplusToken, &gdiplusStartupInput, nullptr) != Ok) {
            return; // Jika GDI+ gagal, lanjut tanpa splash
        }

        wchar_t modulePath[MAX_PATH];
        GetModuleFileNameW(g_hSelfModule ? g_hSelfModule : GetModuleHandleW(nullptr), modulePath, MAX_PATH);
        wchar_t *lastSlash = wcsrchr(modulePath, L'\\');
        if (lastSlash) *lastSlash = L'\0';
        std::wstring imagePath = std::wstring(modulePath) + L"\\logo\\OblivionHS.png";

        HWND hwndSplash = nullptr;
        HBITMAP hBmp = nullptr;
        Bitmap *img = nullptr;
        img = Bitmap::FromFile(imagePath.c_str(), FALSE);
        if (!img || img->GetLastStatus() != Ok) {
            delete img;
            GdiplusShutdown(gdiplusToken);
            return; // Tidak ada gambar, abaikan
        }

        const int imgW = static_cast<int>(img->GetWidth());
        const int imgH = static_cast<int>(img->GetHeight());

        WNDCLASSW wc{};
        wc.lpfnWndProc = [](HWND h, UINT m, WPARAM w, LPARAM l)->LRESULT {
            if (m == WM_PAINT) {
                PAINTSTRUCT ps; HDC hdc = BeginPaint(h, &ps);
                RECT rc; GetClientRect(h, &rc);
                Graphics g(hdc);
                g.SetInterpolationMode(InterpolationModeHighQualityBicubic);
                g.SetPixelOffsetMode(PixelOffsetModeHighQuality);
                g.SetSmoothingMode(SmoothingModeHighQuality);
                // Tarik pointer image dari properti window
                Bitmap* pImg = reinterpret_cast<Bitmap*>(GetPropW(h, L"__OBV_IMG"));
                if (pImg) {
                    // Render image dengan ukuran penuh sesuai client area
                    g.DrawImage(pImg, 0, 0, rc.right, rc.bottom);
                }
                EndPaint(h, &ps);
                return 0;
            }
            return DefWindowProcW(h, m, w, l);
        };
        wc.hInstance = GetModuleHandleW(nullptr);
        wc.hCursor = LoadCursor(nullptr, IDC_ARROW);
        wc.lpszClassName = L"OblivionSplashClass";
        RegisterClassW(&wc);

        // Hitung ukuran window dengan border adjustment untuk WS_POPUP
        RECT rect = {0, 0, imgW, imgH};
        AdjustWindowRectEx(&rect, WS_POPUP, FALSE, WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW);
        int windowW = rect.right - rect.left;
        int windowH = rect.bottom - rect.top;

        // Posisi center layar
        int screenW = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN);
        int screenH = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN);
        int x = (screenW - windowW) / 2;
        int y = (screenH - windowH) / 2;

        hwndSplash = CreateWindowExW(
            WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW,
            wc.lpszClassName,
            L"Oblivion Hack Shield",
            WS_POPUP,
            x, y, windowW, windowH,
            nullptr, nullptr, wc.hInstance, nullptr);

        if (!hwndSplash) {
            delete img;
            GdiplusShutdown(gdiplusToken);
            return;
        }

        SetPropW(hwndSplash, L"__OBV_IMG", reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(img));
        ShowWindow(hwndSplash, SW_SHOW);
        UpdateWindow(hwndSplash);

        // Jalankan message loop singkat selama 2000 ms
        const ULONGLONG durationMs = 2000;
        ULONGLONG start = GetTickCount64();
        MSG msg;
        while (GetTickCount64() - start < durationMs) {
            while (PeekMessageW(&msg, nullptr, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
                TranslateMessage(&msg);
                DispatchMessageW(&msg);
            }
            Sleep(10);
        }

        RemovePropW(hwndSplash, L"__OBV_IMG");
        DestroyWindow(hwndSplash);
        delete img;
        GdiplusShutdown(gdiplusToken);
    };

    // Tampilkan splash sebelum console init
    ShowSplash();

    // --- LANGKAH PERTAMA: BUAT CONSOLE UNTUK DEBUGGING ---
    const bool hideConsole = ShouldHideDebugConsole();
    if (!hideConsole) {
        // Sesuai requirement: client menggunakan Console
        AllocConsole();
        FILE* fDummy;
        freopen_s(&fDummy, "CONOUT$", "w", stdout);
        freopen_s(&fDummy, "CONOUT$", "w", stderr);
        freopen_s(&fDummy, "CONIN$", "r", stdin);
        std::cout.clear();
        std::clog.clear();
        std::cerr.clear();
        std::cin.clear();

        SetConsoleTitleW(L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Debug Console");
        std::cout << "==================================================" << std::endl;
        std::cout << "  OBLIVION HACK SHIELD - Anti-Cheat System" << std::endl;
        std::cout << "  Version 1.0 - Ragnarok Online Protection" << std::endl;
        std::cout << "==================================================" << std::endl;
        std::cout << "[INIT] Console initialized" << std::endl;
    } else {
        OutputDebugStringW(L"[INFO] Oblivion Hack Shield debug console disabled via configuration.");
    }
    
    // --- LANGKAH KEDUA: BUAT NETWORK REPORTER ---
    std::cout << "[INIT] Creating network reporter..." << std::endl;
    try {
        // Ambil secret yang di-obfuscate (bukan string literal langsung)
        const std::string kClientSecret = Oblivion::GetClientSecret();
        // TODO: Ganti "127.0.0.1" dengan IP VPS server Anda
        // Contoh: "192.168.1.100" atau "your-server-ip.com"
        g_pNetworkReporter = new Oblivion::NetworkReporter("127.0.0.1", 54321, kClientSecret);
        std::cout << "[INIT] Network reporter created" << std::endl;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] Failed to create network reporter: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        MessageBoxW(NULL, L"Gagal menginisialisasi network reporter.\n\nClient akan ditutup.", 
                    L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Error", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
        ExitProcess(1);
    } catch (...) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] Unknown error creating network reporter" << std::endl;
        MessageBoxW(NULL, L"Kesalahan tidak diketahui saat inisialisasi.\n\nClient akan ditutup.", 
                    L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Error", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
        ExitProcess(1);
    }

    // --- LANGKAH KETIGA: CEK BAN STATUS DARI SERVER ---
    std::cout << "[INIT] Checking ban status with server..." << std::endl;
    try {
        if (!g_pNetworkReporter->CheckBanStatus()) {
            // Jika di-ban, CheckBanStatus sudah memanggil ExitProcess, jadi tidak akan sampai di sini
            return;
        }
        std::cout << "[INIT] Ban check passed" << std::endl;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] Exception during ban check: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        MessageBoxW(NULL, L"Kesalahan saat memeriksa status ban.\n\nClient akan ditutup.", 
                    L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Error", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
        ExitProcess(1);
    }

    // --- LANGKAH KEEMPAT: PERIKSA INTEGRITAS FILE ---
    std::cout << "[INIT] Performing file integrity check..." << std::endl;
    try {
        Oblivion::FileIntegrityChecker integrityChecker;
        integrityChecker.PerformCheck();
        std::cout << "[INIT] File integrity check passed" << std::endl;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] File integrity check failed: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "FILE_INTEGRITY_EXCEPTION", e.what());
        return;
    } catch (...) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] Unknown error during file integrity check" << std::endl;
        ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "FILE_INTEGRITY_UNKNOWN_ERROR", "Unknown exception");
        return;
    }

    // --- LANGKAH KELIMA: AKTIFKAN SELF-INTEGRITY GUARD ---
    std::cout << "[INIT] Starting self-integrity guard..." << std::endl;
    try {
        if (!g_pSelfIntegrityGuard) {
            g_pSelfIntegrityGuard = new Oblivion::SelfIntegrityGuard(g_hSelfModule);
            if (!g_pSelfIntegrityGuard->Start()) {
                std::cerr << "[ERROR] Self-integrity guard failed to start" << std::endl;
                ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "SELF_GUARD_INIT_FAILED", "Unable to initialize self integrity guard");
                return;
            }
            std::cout << "[INIT] Self-integrity guard active" << std::endl;
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] Exception starting self-integrity guard: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "SELF_GUARD_EXCEPTION", e.what());
        return;
    }

    // --- LANGKAH KEENAM: MULAI SISTEM PEMANTAUAN ---
    std::cout << "[INIT] Starting process watcher..." << std::endl;
    try {
        g_pProcessWatcher = new Oblivion::ProcessWatcher();
        g_pProcessWatcher->Start();
        std::cout << "[SUCCESS] Oblivion Hack Shield fully initialized!" << std::endl;
        std::cout << "[INFO] Protection active - monitoring for threats..." << std::endl;
        std::cout << "==================================================" << std::endl;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        std::cerr << "[ERROR] Exception starting process watcher: " << e.what() << std::endl;
        ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "PROCESS_WATCHER_EXCEPTION", e.what());
        return;
    }
}

// DLL Entry Point
BOOL APIENTRY DllMain(HMODULE hModule, DWORD ul_reason_for_call, LPVOID lpReserved) {
    switch (ul_reason_for_call) {
    case DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH: {
        // Mencegah thread detach notification untuk performa yang lebih baik
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(hModule);
        g_hSelfModule = hModule;

        // PENTING: Jangan melakukan operasi blocking di DllMain!
        // Pindahkan semua inisialisasi berat ke thread terpisah agar aplikasi bisa menampilkan window
        // Buat thread untuk inisialisasi sistem keamanan
        std::thread initThread(InitializeSecuritySystem);
        initThread.detach(); // Detach thread agar bisa berjalan mandiri
        break;
    }

    case DLL_PROCESS_DETACH:
        std::cout << "[CLEANUP] Shutting down Oblivion Hack Shield..." << std::endl;
        
        // Stop process watcher terlebih dahulu (ini akan stop monitoring)
        if (g_pProcessWatcher) {
            std::cout << "[CLEANUP] Stopping process watcher..." << std::endl;
            try {
                g_pProcessWatcher->Stop();
                delete g_pProcessWatcher;
                g_pProcessWatcher = nullptr;
            } catch (...) {
                // Ignore exceptions during cleanup
            }
        }
        
        // Kemudian stop self-integrity guard
        if (g_pSelfIntegrityGuard) {
            std::cout << "[CLEANUP] Stopping self-integrity guard..." << std::endl;
            try {
                g_pSelfIntegrityGuard->Stop();
                delete g_pSelfIntegrityGuard;
                g_pSelfIntegrityGuard = nullptr;
            } catch (...) {
                // Ignore exceptions during cleanup
            }
        }
        
        // Terakhir cleanup network reporter
        if (g_pNetworkReporter) {
            std::cout << "[CLEANUP] Cleaning up network reporter..." << std::endl;
            try {
                delete g_pNetworkReporter;
                g_pNetworkReporter = nullptr;
            } catch (...) {
                // Ignore exceptions during cleanup
            }
        }
        
        std::cout << "[CLEANUP] Shutdown complete" << std::endl;
        break;

    case DLL_THREAD_ATTACH:
    case DLL_THREAD_DETACH:
        break;
    }
    return TRUE;
}
```

`client/include/AntiDebug.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>
#include <string>

namespace Oblivion {
    class AntiDebug {
    public:
        AntiDebug();
        ~AntiDebug();

        // Fungsi utama untuk menjalankan semua pemeriksaan anti-debug
        void PerformCheck();

    private:
        // Metode 1: Menggunakan API IsDebuggerPresent
        bool CheckDebuggerPresentAPI();

        // Metode 2: Menggunakan NtQueryInformationProcess
        bool CheckNtQueryInfoProcess();

        // Metode 3: Jebakan dengan software exception (INT 3)
        bool CheckInt3Trap();

        // Metode 4: Deteksi hardware breakpoint via DR0–DR7
        bool CheckHardwareBreakpoints();

        // Metode 5: Deteksi kernel-mode debugger via SystemKernelDebuggerInformation
        bool CheckKernelDebugger();

        // Metode 6: Deteksi anomali timing akibat debugging/stepping
        bool CheckTimingAnomalies();

        // Metode 7: Validasi integritas fungsi kritis (anti-hook)
        bool CheckApiHookIntegrity();

        // Metode 8: Baca indikator debugger dari PEB
        bool CheckPEBFlags();

        // Metode 9: Integritas rantai SEH (x86) / heuristik x64
        bool CheckSEHChainIntegrity();

        // Metode 10: Integritas VEH (vectored exception handlers)
        bool CheckVEHIntegrity();
    };
}
```

`client/include/advanced_process_heuristics.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>
#include <string>
#include <functional>

namespace Oblivion {
    class AdvancedProcessHeuristics {
    public:
        AdvancedProcessHeuristics();
        ~AdvancedProcessHeuristics();

        void PerformScan();
        
        // Set callback untuk heartbeat update (untuk mencegah timeout di scan yang lama)
        void SetHeartbeatCallback(std::function<void()> callback);

    private:
        AdvancedProcessHeuristics(const AdvancedProcessHeuristics&) = delete;
        AdvancedProcessHeuristics& operator=(const AdvancedProcessHeuristics&) = delete;

        // DLL detection
        bool ContainsSuspiciousModule(DWORD processId, std::wstring& matchedModule) const;
        
        // Window class detection
        bool HasSuspiciousWindowClass(DWORD processId, std::wstring& matchedClass) const;
        
        // Window title pattern detection
        bool HasSuspiciousWindowTitle(DWORD processId, std::wstring& matchedPattern) const;
        
        // Path-based detection
        bool HasSuspiciousPath(DWORD processId, std::wstring& matchedPath) const;
        
        // Handle-based detection (ULTIMATE - detects any memory scanner)
        bool HasSuspiciousHandleToGame(DWORD gameProcessId, std::wstring& suspiciousProcess) const;
        
        // Behavioral detection
        bool HasSuspiciousBehavior(DWORD processId, std::wstring& behaviorType) const;
        
        // Memory signature detection
        bool HasSuspiciousMemorySignature(DWORD processId, std::wstring& matchedPattern) const;

        // Helper functions
        std::string BuildHeuristicDetails(const std::wstring& processName,
                                          DWORD processId,
                                          const std::wstring& detectionType,
                                          const std::wstring& indicator) const;

        std::string ConvertWideToUtf8(const std::wstring& input) const;
        
        // Heartbeat callback untuk mencegah timeout
        std::function<void()> m_heartbeatCallback;
    };

    // Global helper functions for advanced heuristics
    bool ShouldPerformMemoryScan(const std::wstring& processName);
    bool IsSuspiciousBehavior(DWORD processId);
    void LogSuspiciousProcess(const std::wstring& processName, DWORD processId, const std::string& reason);
}
```

```h
        void GuardianLoop();

        std::thread m_guardianThread;
```

`client/include/digital_signature_validator.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <map>

namespace Oblivion {
    class DigitalSignatureValidator {
    public:
        DigitalSignatureValidator();
        ~DigitalSignatureValidator();

        // Fungsi utama untuk memindai semua modul
        void PerformScan();

        // Fungsi untuk verifikasi process executable (untuk whitelist verification)
        // Mengembalikan true jika signature valid dan publisher sesuai ekspektasi
        static bool VerifyProcessSignature(const std::wstring& processPath, const std::wstring& expectedPublisher);
        
        // Fungsi untuk mendapatkan publisher dari file executable
        static bool GetFilePublisher(const std::wstring& filePath, std::wstring& publisherName);

        // Public access ke expected publishers map untuk cross-verification
        static const std::map<std::wstring, std::wstring> m_expectedPublishers;

    private:
        // Fungsi untuk memeriksa apakah sebuah modul tepercaya
        bool IsModuleTrusted(HMODULE hModule, std::wstring& moduleName);

        // Fungsi untuk mendapatkan nama penerbit dari sertifikat
        bool GetCertificateSubjectName(HMODULE hModule, std::wstring& subjectName);

        // Fungsi helper untuk verifikasi signature file
        static bool VerifyFileSignature(const std::wstring& filePath);

        // Daftar penerbit yang dipercaya
        static const std::vector<std::wstring> m_trustedPublishers;
        
        // Daftar DLL yang diizinkan meskipun tidak ditandatangani (DLL legitimate dari game/engine)
        static const std::vector<std::wstring> m_allowedUnsignedDlls;
    };
}
```

`client/include/file_integrity_check.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace Oblivion {
    class FileIntegrityChecker {
    public:
        // Fungsi utama untuk menjalankan semua pemeriksaan file
        void PerformCheck();

    private:
        // Struktur untuk menyimpan pasangan nama file dan hash yang diharapkan
        struct FileHashPair {
            std::wstring relativePath; // Path relatif dari folder client
            std::string expectedHash;   // Hash SHA-256 dalam format hex
        };

        // Fungsi untuk menghitung hash SHA-256 dari sebuah file
        std::string CalculateSHA256(const std::wstring& filePath);

        // Daftar file yang harus diperiksa integritasnya
        static const std::vector<FileHashPair> m_filesToCheck;
    };
}
```

`client/include/heartbeat_protocol.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

namespace Oblivion {
    // Heartbeat message types
    enum class HbType : uint8_t { Ping = 1, Pong = 2 };

    // Client runtime status bits
    struct ClientStatus {
        bool antiSuspendOk;   // true if no suspend detected
        bool integrityOk;     // true if self-integrity guard passes
    };

    // Wire payload (serialized as: TYPE|SEQ|TS|NONCE|STATUS|HMAC;) for now
    struct HeartbeatMessage {
        HbType type;
        uint32_t seq;           // monotonically increasing sequence
        uint64_t t_send_ms;     // server/client wall-clock in ms
        uint64_t nonce;         // server-provided random for Ping; echoed by Pong
        ClientStatus status;    // client only
        std::string hmac;       // hex string of HMAC-SHA256 over fields
    };

    // Default timing
    struct HeartbeatConfig {
        uint32_t interval_ms = 3000;   // target interval
        uint32_t jitter_ms = 250;      // +- jitter
        uint32_t timeout_ms = 4000;    // receive timeout per hop
        uint32_t max_consecutive_miss = 3; // misses before warning/flag
        uint32_t rtt_warn_ms = 1500;   // sustained RTT limit to warn
    };
}
```

`client/include/memory_scanner.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <windows.h>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <cstdint>
#include <fstream>
#include <map>
#include <unordered_set>

namespace Oblivion {
    // Struktur untuk menyimpan baseline yang diverifikasi dari disk
    struct ExecutableRegion {
        uintptr_t rva;              // Relative Virtual Address (offset dari base)
        size_t size;                // Ukuran region dalam bytes
        uint32_t diskCRC32;         // CRC32 dari file di disk (ground truth)
        uint32_t memoryCRC32;       // CRC32 dari memori saat pertama load (untuk cross-check)
        bool isCodeSection;         // Apakah ini code section (.text)
    };

    // Struktur untuk PE section header info
    struct PESectionInfo {
        std::string name{};
        uint32_t virtualAddress{0};
        uint32_t virtualSize{0};
        uint32_t rawDataOffset{0};
        uint32_t rawDataSize{0};
        uint32_t characteristics{0};
    };

    // Embedded signature (diisi saat build-time)
    struct EmbeddedSignature {
        uint32_t magicNumber;       // Magic number untuk validasi
        uint32_t textSectionHash;   // SHA256 hash dari .text section (first 32 bits)
        uint32_t rdataSectionHash;  // SHA256 hash dari .rdata section
        uint32_t buildTimestamp;    // Timestamp saat build
        uint32_t imageSize;         // Total size dari PE image
        uint32_t checksum;          // Checksum untuk memverifikasi signature ini
    };

    class MemoryScanner {
    public:
        MemoryScanner();
        ~MemoryScanner();

        // Fungsi utama untuk memindai memori
        void PerformScan();

    private:
        // === LAYER 1: PE Header & Structure Validation ===
        bool ValidatePEStructure(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData);
        bool ParsePEHeaders(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData, 
                          std::vector<PESectionInfo>& sections);
        bool ValidateImageHeaders(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData,
                                 IMAGE_DOS_HEADER& dosHeader,
                                 IMAGE_NT_HEADERS& ntHeaders);
        
        // === LAYER 2: Disk-Based Baseline Creation ===
        bool ReadExecutableFile(const std::wstring& filePath, std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData);
        void CreateBaselineFromDisk(const std::wstring& filePath);
        uint32_t CalculateSectionHashFromDisk(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData,
                                             const PESectionInfo& section);
        
        // === LAYER 3: Embedded Signature Verification ===
        bool VerifyEmbeddedSignature(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData);
        bool ExtractEmbeddedSignature(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData,
                                     EmbeddedSignature& signature);
        uint32_t CalculateSignatureChecksum(const EmbeddedSignature& sig);
        
        // === LAYER 4: Cross-Verification (Disk vs Memory) ===
        bool CrossVerifyDiskAndMemory(const std::vector<uint8_t>& fileData);
        bool CompareCodeSections(const std::vector<uint8_t>& diskData,
                                const uint8_t* memoryBase,
                                const PESectionInfo& section);
        
        // === LAYER 5: Runtime Integrity Verification ===
        bool VerifyRegionIntegrity(uint8_t* memoryBase, size_t regionSize, uintptr_t rva);
        bool DetectSuspiciousMemoryRegion(uint8_t* memoryBase, size_t regionSize);
        bool DetectCodeCaveInjection(const uint8_t* codeSection, size_t size);
        
        // === Cryptographic Functions ===
        uint32_t CalculateCRC32(const uint8_t* data, size_t size);
        uint64_t CalculateSHA256_32bit(const uint8_t* data, size_t size);
        
        // === Anti-Tampering Detection ===
        bool DetectBaselineModification();
        bool VerifyBaselineIntegrity();
        uint32_t CalculateBaselineChecksum();

        // === Helpers ===
        const PESectionInfo* GetSectionByRva(uintptr_t rva) const;
        bool IsJITRuntimePresent() const;
        bool IsSystemModule(const std::wstring& modulePath) const;
        bool IsSectionVerified(uint32_t sectionRva) const;

        // === Member Variables ===
        std::vector<ExecutableRegion> m_diskBaseline;      // Baseline dari disk (trusted)
        std::map<uint32_t, PESectionInfo> m_sectionMap;    // Map RVA -> Section Info
        std::unordered_set<uint32_t> m_verifiedSections;   // Sections yang terakhir diverifikasi
        EmbeddedSignature m_embeddedSig;                   // Embedded signature
        uintptr_t m_moduleBaseAddress;                     // Base address modul
        bool m_baselineCreated;                            // Flag baseline creation
        bool m_baselineVerified;                           // Flag baseline verification
        uint32_t m_baselineChecksum;                       // Checksum untuk baseline integrity
        std::wstring m_executablePath;                     // Path ke executable
    };
}
```

`client/src/AntiDebug.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "AntiDebug.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <winternl.h> // Untuk fungsi-fungsi NT yang tidak berdokumentasi

// Forward declaration
extern void ReportViolationAndExit(const std::wstring& message, const std::string& type, const std::string& details);

// Forward declare helper to silence missing identifier errors (definition appears later)
static bool IsModuleWhitelisted(HMODULE hMod);

// Fungsi pointer untuk NtQueryInformationProcess
typedef NTSTATUS(NTAPI* pNtQueryInformationProcess)(
    IN HANDLE ProcessHandle,
    IN PROCESSINFOCLASS ProcessInformationClass,
    OUT PVOID ProcessInformation,
    IN ULONG ProcessInformationLength,
    OUT PULONG ReturnLength OPTIONAL
    );

// VEH probe handler and flag (file-scope)
static volatile LONG gVehCalled = 0;
static LONG CALLBACK VehProbeHandler(PEXCEPTION_POINTERS p) {
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(p);
    InterlockedExchange(&gVehCalled, 1);
    // Swallow exception to avoid crash during probe
    return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION;
}

static bool IsAntiDebugDisabled() {
    wchar_t buf[8] = {0};
    DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"OBLIVION_ANTIDEBUG", buf, 8);
    if (n == 0 || n >= 8) return false;
    if (_wcsicmp(buf, L"0") == 0 || _wcsicmp(buf, L"false") == 0 || _wcsicmp(buf, L"off") == 0) return true;
    return false;
}

static bool IsStrictAntiDebug() {
    wchar_t buf[8] = {0};
    DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"OBLIVION_ANTIDEBUG_STRICT", buf, 8);
    if (n == 0 || n >= 8) return false;
    if (_wcsicmp(buf, L"1") == 0 || _wcsicmp(buf, L"true") == 0 || _wcsicmp(buf, L"on") == 0) return true;
    return false;
}

static bool ShouldLogAntiDebug() {
    wchar_t buf[8] = {0};
    DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"OBLIVION_ANTIDEBUG_LOG", buf, 8);
    if (n == 0 || n >= 8) return false;
    return (_wcsicmp(buf, L"1") == 0 || _wcsicmp(buf, L"true") == 0 || _wcsicmp(buf, L"on") == 0);
}

static void LogAntiDebugEvent(const std::string& details) {
    if (!ShouldLogAntiDebug()) return;

    wchar_t exePath[MAX_PATH] = {0};
    wchar_t logDir[MAX_PATH] = {0};
    DWORD dn = GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"OBLIVION_ANTIDEBUG_LOG_DIR", logDir, MAX_PATH);
    if (dn > 0 && dn < MAX_PATH) {
        // Ensure directory exists (best-effort)
        DWORD attrs = GetFileAttributesW(logDir);
        if (attrs == INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) {
            CreateDirectoryW(logDir, NULL);
        }
        // Build path: <logDir>\antidebug.log
        wcsncpy_s(exePath, logDir, _TRUNCATE);
        size_t len = wcslen(exePath);
        if (len > 0 && exePath[len - 1] != L'\\' && exePath[len - 1] != L'/') {
            wcscat_s(exePath, L"\\");
        }
        wcscat_s(exePath, L"antidebug.log");
    } else {
        if (!GetModuleFileNameW(NULL, exePath, MAX_PATH)) return;
        // Build log path next to the executable
        wchar_t* lastSlash = wcsrchr(exePath, L'\\');
        if (!lastSlash) return;
        *(lastSlash + 1) = L'\0';
        wcscat_s(exePath, L"antidebug.log");
    }

    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(exePath, GENERIC_WRITE, FILE_SHARE_READ, NULL, OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, NULL);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;

    // Move to end for append
    SetFilePointer(h, 0, NULL, FILE_END);

    SYSTEMTIME st; GetLocalTime(&st);
    char timeBuf[64];
    int len = sprintf_s(timeBuf, "%04d-%02d-%02d %02d:%02d:%02d ", st.wYear, st.wMonth, st.wDay, st.wHour, st.wMinute, st.wSecond);
    DWORD written = 0;
    WriteFile(h, timeBuf, (DWORD)len, &written, NULL);
    WriteFile(h, details.c_str(), (DWORD)details.size(), &written, NULL);
    const char* nl = "\r\n";
    WriteFile(h, nl, 2, &written, NULL);

    CloseHandle(h);
}

namespace Oblivion {
    AntiDebug::AntiDebug() {}

    AntiDebug::~AntiDebug() {}

    void AntiDebug::PerformCheck() {
        if (IsAntiDebugDisabled()) {
            return;
        }

        bool strict = IsStrictAntiDebug();
        int hits = 0;
        std::string details;

        auto add = [&](const char* name, bool v) {
            if (v) {
                if (!details.empty()) details += ";";
                details += name;
                ++hits;
            }
            if (strict && v) {
                LogAntiDebugEvent(details);
                ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "DEBUGGER_DETECTED", details);
            }
        };

        add("IsDebuggerPresent", CheckDebuggerPresentAPI());
        add("NtQueryInfoProcess(DebugPort)", CheckNtQueryInfoProcess());
        add("INT3Trap", CheckInt3Trap());
        add("HWBreakpoints", CheckHardwareBreakpoints());
        add("KernelDebugger", CheckKernelDebugger());
        add("TimingAnomalies", CheckTimingAnomalies());
        add("ApiHookIntegrity", CheckApiHookIntegrity());
        add("PEBFlags", CheckPEBFlags());
        add("SEHChainIntegrity", CheckSEHChainIntegrity());
        add("VEHIntegrity", CheckVEHIntegrity());

        if (hits >= 2 && !details.empty()) {
            LogAntiDebugEvent(details);
            ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "DEBUGGER_DETECTED", details);
        }
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckDebuggerPresentAPI() {
        // Metode paling sederhana, mudah di-bypass tapi wajib ada.
        return IsDebuggerPresent() != FALSE;
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckNtQueryInfoProcess() {
        HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleW(L"ntdll.dll");
        if (!hNtdll) {
            return false; // Tidak bisa mendapatkan handle, asumsikan aman
        }

        pNtQueryInformationProcess NtQueryInfoProc = (pNtQueryInformationProcess)GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtQueryInformationProcess");
        if (!NtQueryInfoProc) {
            return false; // Fungsi tidak ditemukan, asumsikan aman
        }

        DWORD processDebugPort = 0;
        NTSTATUS status = NtQueryInfoProc(
            GetCurrentProcess(),
            ProcessDebugPort, // Kelas informasi: 0x7
            &processDebugPort,
            sizeof(DWORD),
            NULL
        );

        // Jika status berhasil dan port debug bukan nol, debugger terdeteksi.
        if (status == 0x00000000 && processDebugPort != 0) {
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckInt3Trap() {
        // Metode yang sangat kuat.
        // Kita mencoba menjalankan __debugbreak() yang memicu INT 3.
        // Jika debugger ada, ia akan menangkap exception.
        // Jika tidak, exception handler kita (dalam __except) yang akan menanganinya.
        __try {
            __debugbreak(); // Ini setara dengan int 3
        }
        __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
            // Kode di dalam blok ini hanya dijalankan jika TIDAK ADA debugger.
            // Jika kita sampai di sini, berarti aman.
            return false;
        }

        // Jika eksekusi berlanjut ke sini, berarti exception ditangkap oleh debugger,
        // bukan oleh handler kita.
        return true;
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckHardwareBreakpoints() {
        // Cek DR0–DR3 dan flag enable di DR7 pada thread saat ini
        CONTEXT ctx = {};
        ctx.ContextFlags = CONTEXT_DEBUG_REGISTERS;
        if (!GetThreadContext(GetCurrentThread(), &ctx)) {
            return false; // Tidak bisa mendapatkan konteks, asumsikan aman
        }

        bool anyDrSet = (ctx.Dr0 != 0) || (ctx.Dr1 != 0) || (ctx.Dr2 != 0) || (ctx.Dr3 != 0);
        bool dr7Enabled = (ctx.Dr7 & 0xFF) != 0; // Enable bits untuk local/global bp
        return anyDrSet || dr7Enabled;
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckKernelDebugger() {
        // Query SystemKernelDebuggerInformation untuk melihat apakah kernel debugger aktif
        typedef NTSTATUS (NTAPI* pNtQuerySystemInformation)(
            ULONG SystemInformationClass,
            PVOID SystemInformation,
            ULONG SystemInformationLength,
            PULONG ReturnLength
        );

        HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleW(L"ntdll.dll");
        if (!hNtdll) return false;
        auto NtQuerySystemInformation = (pNtQuerySystemInformation)GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtQuerySystemInformation");
        if (!NtQuerySystemInformation) return false;

        struct KDI { // Struktur kecil untuk Kernel Debugger Info (bools)
            BOOLEAN KernelDebuggerEnabled;
            BOOLEAN KernelDebuggerNotPresent;
        } info = {0};

        // Nilai kelas 35 biasanya SystemKernelDebuggerInformation pada banyak build
        const ULONG SystemKernelDebuggerInformation = 35;
        NTSTATUS status = NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemKernelDebuggerInformation, &info, sizeof(info), nullptr);
        if (status != 0x00000000) {
            return false;
        }

        // Jika kernel debugger enabled dan tidak dianggap not present, anggap terdeteksi
        if (info.KernelDebuggerEnabled && !info.KernelDebuggerNotPresent) {
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckTimingAnomalies() {
        // Baseline adaptif + beberapa sampling untuk mengurangi false positive
        LARGE_INTEGER f = {0};
        if (!QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f)) return false;

        auto sample = [&](int iters) -> double {
            LARGE_INTEGER s = {0}, e = {0};
            if (!QueryPerformanceCounter(&s)) return 0.0;
            for (volatile int i = 0; i < iters; ++i) {
            }
            if (!QueryPerformanceCounter(&e)) return 0.0;
            return (double)(e.QuadPart - s.QuadPart) * 1000.0 / (double)f.QuadPart;
        };

        static double baselineMs = 0.0;
        if (baselineMs <= 0.0) {
            // Hitung baseline dari beberapa sampel ringan
            const int iters = 100000;
            const int n = 3;
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) sum += sample(iters);
            baselineMs = sum / n;
            if (baselineMs <= 0.0) baselineMs = 10.0; // fallback konservatif
        }

        // Ambang adaptif: baseline * faktor + margin
        const double factor = 2.5; // debugger/stepping biasanya menggelembungkan waktu signifikan
        const double marginMs = 5.0;
        const double thresholdMs = baselineMs * factor + marginMs;

        // Lakukan beberapa sampel; jika mayoritas melewati ambang, anggap anomali
        int anomalies = 0;
        const int checks = 3;
        for (int i = 0; i < checks; ++i) {
            double t = sample(100000);
            if (t > thresholdMs) ++anomalies;
        }
        return anomalies >= 2; // konsensus sederhana
    }

    static bool IsAddressInModule(HMODULE mod, void* addr) {
        if (!mod || !addr) return false;
        auto dos = (PIMAGE_DOS_HEADER)mod;
        if (dos->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) return false;
        auto nt = (PIMAGE_NT_HEADERS)((BYTE*)mod + dos->e_lfanew);
        if (nt->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) return false;
        SIZE_T size = nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage;
        return (addr >= (void*)mod) && ((BYTE*)addr < ((BYTE*)mod + size));
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckApiHookIntegrity() {
        // Validasi bahwa IsDebuggerPresent dan NtQueryInformationProcess menunjuk ke modul asli
        HMODULE hKernel32 = GetModuleHandleW(L"kernel32.dll");
        HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleW(L"ntdll.dll");
        if (!hKernel32 || !hNtdll) return false;

        FARPROC pIsDbg = GetProcAddress(hKernel32, "IsDebuggerPresent");
        FARPROC pNtQIP = GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtQueryInformationProcess");
        if (!pIsDbg || !pNtQIP) return false;

        // Jika alamat fungsi berada di luar modul aslinya, indikasi hooking (trampolin)
        bool isDbgHooked = !IsAddressInModule(hKernel32, (void*)pIsDbg);
        bool ntqipHooked = !IsAddressInModule(hNtdll, (void*)pNtQIP);

        // Tambahan: cek prolog untuk lompatan tidak biasa (heuristik sederhana)
        auto bytes = (BYTE*)pNtQIP;
        bool suspiciousJmp = (bytes[0] == 0xE9) || (bytes[0] == 0xEA) || (bytes[0] == 0xFF); // jmp/call absolute/indirect

        // Whitelist suppression
        if (isDbgHooked) {
            HMODULE actual = nullptr;
            if (GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                                   (LPCWSTR)pIsDbg, &actual)) {
                if (actual && IsModuleWhitelisted(actual)) {
                    isDbgHooked = false;
                }
            }
        }
        if (ntqipHooked || suspiciousJmp) {
            HMODULE actual = nullptr;
            if (GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                                   (LPCWSTR)pNtQIP, &actual)) {
                if (actual && IsModuleWhitelisted(actual)) {
                    ntqipHooked = false;
                    suspiciousJmp = false;
                }
            }
        }

        bool suspicious = isDbgHooked || ntqipHooked || suspiciousJmp;
        if (suspicious && ShouldLogAntiDebug()) {
            // Log detail modul asal alamat jika bukan modul asli
            auto logAddr = [&](const char* name, HMODULE expected, FARPROC addr) {
                HMODULE actual = nullptr;
                if (GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                                       (LPCWSTR)addr, &actual)) {
                    wchar_t modName[MAX_PATH] = {0};
                    GetModuleFileNameW(actual, modName, MAX_PATH);
                    char utf8[512] = {0};
                    WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, modName, -1, utf8, sizeof(utf8)-1, NULL, NULL);
                    char msg[768];
                    sprintf_s(msg, sizeof(msg), "APIHook %s addr=%p module=%s (expected=%p)", name, addr, utf8, expected);
                    LogAntiDebugEvent(std::string(msg));
                } else {
                    char msg[256];
                    sprintf_s(msg, sizeof(msg), "APIHook %s addr=%p (module unknown)", name, addr);
                    LogAntiDebugEvent(std::string(msg));
                }
            };

            if (isDbgHooked) logAddr("IsDebuggerPresent", hKernel32, pIsDbg);
            if (ntqipHooked || suspiciousJmp) logAddr("NtQueryInformationProcess", hNtdll, pNtQIP);
        }
        return suspicious;
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckPEBFlags() {
        // Baca BeingDebugged dan NtGlobalFlag dari PEB via NtQueryInformationProcess
        typedef NTSTATUS(NTAPI* pNtQueryInformationProcess)(HANDLE, PROCESSINFOCLASS, PVOID, ULONG, PULONG);
        HMODULE hNtdll = GetModuleHandleW(L"ntdll.dll");
        if (!hNtdll) return false;
        auto NtQueryInfoProc = (pNtQueryInformationProcess)GetProcAddress(hNtdll, "NtQueryInformationProcess");
        if (!NtQueryInfoProc) return false;

        // Ambil ProcessBasicInformation untuk mendapatkan PEB
        struct PROCESS_BASIC_INFORMATION_LOCAL {
            PVOID Reserved1;
            PVOID PebBaseAddress;
            PVOID Reserved2[2];
            ULONG UniqueProcessId;
            PVOID Reserved3;
        } pbi = {};

        NTSTATUS st = NtQueryInfoProc(GetCurrentProcess(), ProcessBasicInformation, &pbi, sizeof(pbi), nullptr);
        if (st != 0x00000000 || !pbi.PebBaseAddress) return false;

        // Struktur PEB minimal untuk membaca BeingDebugged dan NtGlobalFlag
        struct PEB_MIN {
            BYTE Reserved1[2];
            BYTE BeingDebugged;
            BYTE Reserved2[1];
            PVOID Reserved3[2];
            PVOID Ldr;
            PVOID ProcessParameters;
            BYTE Reserved4[104];
            ULONG NtGlobalFlag;
        };

        auto peb = (PEB_MIN*)pbi.PebBaseAddress;
        __try {
            bool dbg = peb->BeingDebugged != 0;
            // NtGlobalFlag sering memiliki 0x70 jika di bawah debugger (FLG_HEAP_ENABLE_TAIL_CHECK | FLG_HEAP_ENABLE_FREE_CHECK)
            bool ngf = (peb->NtGlobalFlag & 0x70) != 0;
            return dbg || ngf;
        }
        __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckSEHChainIntegrity() {
        // Catatan: Pada x86, rantai SEH berada di FS:[0]. Pada x64, SEH tidak menggunakan linked list yang sama.
        // Implementasi: x86 → validasi panjang rantai dan modul handler; x64 → heuristik lemah (return false).
#if defined(_M_IX86)
        struct EXCEPTION_REGISTRATION_RECORD {
            EXCEPTION_REGISTRATION_RECORD* Next;
            PVOID Handler;
        };

        EXCEPTION_REGISTRATION_RECORD* p = nullptr;
        __asm {
            mov eax, dword ptr fs:[0]
            mov p, eax
        }

        int depth = 0;
        const int maxDepth = 64; // kedalaman wajar; terlalu dalam bisa indikasi hooking aneh
        while (p && p != (EXCEPTION_REGISTRATION_RECORD*)0xFFFFFFFF && depth < maxDepth) {
            // Validasi handler berada dalam modul proses (ntdll/kernel32/our module)
            HMODULE hMod = nullptr;
            if (GetModuleHandleExA(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                                   (LPCSTR)p->Handler, &hMod)) {
                // Jika handler berada di modul yang tidak dikenal, anggap mencurigakan
                wchar_t modName[MAX_PATH] = {0};
                GetModuleFileNameW(hMod, modName, MAX_PATH);
                bool allowed = (wcsstr(modName, L"ntdll.dll") != nullptr) ||
                               (wcsstr(modName, L"kernel32.dll") != nullptr);
                HMODULE hSelf = nullptr;
                if (GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                                       (LPCWSTR)&IsAddressInModule, &hSelf)) {
                    if (hSelf && hMod == hSelf) {
                        allowed = true;
                    }
                }
                // Cek modul kita (client dll) jika diketahui nama
                // Jika tidak termasuk yang diizinkan, flag
                if (!allowed) {
                    if (ShouldLogAntiDebug()) {
                        std::string msg = "SEH suspicious module: ";
                        char utf8[512] = {0};
                        WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, modName, -1, utf8, sizeof(utf8)-1, NULL, NULL);
                        msg += utf8;
                        LogAntiDebugEvent(msg);
                    }
                    return true; // integritas SEH mencurigakan
                }
            }

            p = p->Next;
            ++depth;
        }

        if (depth >= maxDepth) {
            if (ShouldLogAntiDebug()) LogAntiDebugEvent("SEH chain too deep");
            return true; // rantai terlalu dalam → indikasi manipulasi
        }
        return false;
#else
        // Pada x64, tidak ada linked-list SEH tradisional; skip dengan aman
        return false;
#endif
    }

    bool AntiDebug::CheckVEHIntegrity() {
        // Pasang VEH sementara, picu exception, dan verifikasi handler kita dipanggil.
        // Jika tidak, kemungkinan ada VEH lain yang menelan exception secara tidak wajar.
        // Reset flag sebelum test
        InterlockedExchange(&gVehCalled, 0);

        // Tahap 1: pasang dengan prioritas lebih rendah (1) agar handler lain yang lebih tinggi bisa jalan
        PVOID hVeh = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1, (PVECTORED_EXCEPTION_HANDLER)VehProbeHandler);
        if (!hVeh) {
            return false;
        }
        RaiseException(0xE0424242, 0, 0, nullptr); // benign custom exception; swallowed by our handler if reached

        RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(hVeh);

        if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&gVehCalled, 0, 0) == 0) {
            // Tahap 2: retry dengan prioritas tertinggi (0).
            // Jika pada prioritas 0 handler kita dipanggil, anggap benign (whitelist kemungkinan handler dari modul sendiri).
            InterlockedExchange(&gVehCalled, 0);
            PVOID hVeh2 = AddVectoredExceptionHandler(0, (PVECTORED_EXCEPTION_HANDLER)VehProbeHandler);
            if (!hVeh2) return false;
            RaiseException(0xE0424242, 0, 0, nullptr);
            RemoveVectoredExceptionHandler(hVeh2);
            // Jika tetap tidak dipanggil pada prioritas tertinggi, flag mencurigakan
            bool suspicious = (InterlockedCompareExchange(&gVehCalled, 0, 0) == 0);
            if (suspicious && ShouldLogAntiDebug()) LogAntiDebugEvent("VEH suspicious: handler swallowed exceptions at all priorities");
            return suspicious;
        }

        // Dipanggil pada prioritas 1 → integritas oke
        return false;
    }
}
static bool IsModuleWhitelisted(HMODULE hMod) {
    wchar_t wl[1024] = {0};
    DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"OBLIVION_HOOK_WHITELIST", wl, 1024);
    if (n == 0 || n >= 1024) return false;

    wchar_t modPath[MAX_PATH] = {0};
    if (!GetModuleFileNameW(hMod, modPath, MAX_PATH)) return false;
    wchar_t* fname = wcsrchr(modPath, L'\\');
    fname = fname ? (fname + 1) : modPath;

    wchar_t fnameLower[MAX_PATH] = {0};
    wcsncpy_s(fnameLower, fname, _TRUNCATE);
    _wcslwr_s(fnameLower, wcslen(fnameLower) + 1);

    wchar_t* ctx = nullptr;
    wchar_t* token = wcstok_s(wl, L";", &ctx);
    while (token) {
        while (*token == L' ' || *token == L'\t') ++token;
        size_t len = wcslen(token);
        while (len && (token[len-1] == L' ' || token[len-1] == L'\t')) { token[--len] = L'\0'; }
        _wcslwr_s(token, len + 1);
        if (len && wcsstr(fnameLower, token) != nullptr) {
            return true;
        }
        token = wcstok_s(nullptr, L";", &ctx);
    }
    return false;
}
```

```cpp
namespace {
    // Cache untuk signature verification - menghindari re-verify proses yang sama
    // Key: Process Path (lowercase), Value: Is Verified (true/false)

    DWORD GetProtectedProcessId() {
        return GetCurrentProcessId();
    }

    std::wstring ToLowerCopy(const std::wstring& input) {
        std::wstring lowered = input;
        std::transform(lowered.begin(), lowered.end(), lowered.begin(), [](wchar_t ch) {
            return static_cast<wchar_t>(towlower(ch));
            });
        return lowered;
    }

    struct WindowSearchContext {

    // Cheat Engine DLL signatures - komprehensif untuk berbagai versi CE

    const std::vector<std::wstring> kSystemProcessWhitelist = {
        // Windows Core Processes

    // Proses yang HARUS diverifikasi dengan digital signature (tidak boleh di-spoof)

    const std::vector<std::wstring> kSignatureVerificationRequired = {
        L"chrome.exe",
        L"firefox.exe",
        L"msedge.exe",
        L"msedgewebview2.exe",  // Microsoft Edge WebView2 Runtime
        L"discord.exe",

        // karena sering unsigned (open-source/freeware)
```
