`client/src/hwid_generator.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "hwid_generator.h"
#include <intrin.h> // Untuk __cpuid
#include <vector>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <bcrypt.h> // Untuk hashing
#include <comdef.h> // Untuk _bstr_t
#include <Wbemidl.h>

// Definisi NTSTATUS jika ntstatus.h tidak tersedia
#ifndef STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL
#define STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL ((NTSTATUS)0xC0000001L)
#endif

#pragma comment(lib, "bcrypt.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "wbemuuid.lib") // Untuk WMI
#pragma comment(lib, "ole32.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "oleaut32.lib")

namespace Oblivion {
    std::string HWIDGenerator::Generate() {
        std::string cpuBrand = GetCpuBrand();
        std::string motherboardUuid = GetWmiProperty("Win32_BaseBoard", "UUID");
        std::string hddSerial = GetWmiProperty("Win32_DiskDrive", "SerialNumber");

        // Ambil hanya drive pertama untuk kesederhanaan
        if (hddSerial.find(",") != std::string::npos) {
            hddSerial = hddSerial.substr(0, hddSerial.find(","));
        }

        std::string combined = cpuBrand + motherboardUuid + hddSerial;
        return HashString(combined);
    }

    std::string HWIDGenerator::GetCpuBrand() {
        int cpuInfo[4] = { -1 };
        char cpuBrandString[0x40] = { 0 };

        // Dapatkan panjang string brand
        __cpuid(cpuInfo, 0x80000000);
        unsigned int nExIds = cpuInfo[0];

        // Dapatkan string brand
        memset(cpuBrandString, 0, sizeof(cpuBrandString));
        for (unsigned int i = 0x80000000; i <= nExIds; ++i) {
            __cpuid(cpuInfo, i);
            if (i == 0x80000002) memcpy(cpuBrandString, cpuInfo, sizeof(cpuInfo));
            else if (i == 0x80000003) memcpy(cpuBrandString + 16, cpuInfo, sizeof(cpuInfo));
            else if (i == 0x80000004) memcpy(cpuBrandString + 32, cpuInfo, sizeof(cpuInfo));
        }
        return std::string(cpuBrandString);
    }

    std::string HWIDGenerator::GetWmiProperty(const std::string& wmiClass, const std::string& wmiProperty) {
        HRESULT hres;
        std::string result = "UNKNOWN";

        // 1. Inisialisasi COM
        hres = CoInitializeEx(0, COINIT_MULTITHREADED);
        if (FAILED(hres)) return result;

        // 2. Set security levels COM
        hres = CoInitializeSecurity(NULL, -1, NULL, NULL, RPC_C_AUTHN_LEVEL_DEFAULT, RPC_C_IMP_LEVEL_IMPERSONATE, NULL, EOAC_NONE, NULL);
        if (FAILED(hres) && hres != RPC_E_TOO_LATE) { CoUninitialize(); return result; }

        // 3. Dapatkan pointer ke WbemLocator
        IWbemLocator* pLoc = NULL;
        hres = CoCreateInstance(CLSID_WbemLocator, 0, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER, IID_IWbemLocator, (LPVOID*)&pLoc);
        if (FAILED(hres)) { CoUninitialize(); return result; }

        // 4. Hubungkan ke WMI
        IWbemServices* pSvc = NULL;
        hres = pLoc->ConnectServer(_bstr_t(L"ROOT\\CIMV2"), NULL, NULL, 0, NULL, 0, 0, &pSvc);
        if (FAILED(hres)) { pLoc->Release(); CoUninitialize(); return result; }

        // 5. Set security levels untuk WMI connection
        hres = CoSetProxyBlanket(pSvc, RPC_C_AUTHN_WINNT, RPC_C_AUTHZ_NONE, NULL, RPC_C_AUTHN_LEVEL_CALL, RPC_C_IMP_LEVEL_IMPERSONATE, NULL, EOAC_NONE);
        if (FAILED(hres)) { pSvc->Release(); pLoc->Release(); CoUninitialize(); return result; }

        // 6. Gunakan WMI query
        IEnumWbemClassObject* pEnumerator = NULL;
        std::wstring query = L"SELECT " + std::wstring(wmiProperty.begin(), wmiProperty.end()) + L" FROM " + std::wstring(wmiClass.begin(), wmiClass.end());
        hres = pSvc->ExecQuery(bstr_t("WQL"), bstr_t(query.c_str()), WBEM_FLAG_FORWARD_ONLY | WBEM_FLAG_RETURN_IMMEDIATELY, NULL, &pEnumerator);
        if (FAILED(hres)) { pSvc->Release(); pLoc->Release(); CoUninitialize(); return result; }

        // 7. Ambil data dari query
        IWbemClassObject* pclsObj = NULL;
        ULONG uReturn = 0;
        while (pEnumerator) {
            HRESULT hr = pEnumerator->Next(WBEM_INFINITE, 1, &pclsObj, &uReturn);
            if (0 == uReturn) break;

            VARIANT vtProp;
            VariantInit(&vtProp); // Inisialisasi VARIANT sebelum digunakan
            hr = pclsObj->Get(_bstr_t(wmiProperty.c_str()), 0, &vtProp, 0, 0);
            if (SUCCEEDED(hr) && vtProp.vt == VT_BSTR) {
                result = _bstr_t(vtProp.bstrVal);
            }
            VariantClear(&vtProp);
            pclsObj->Release();
            break; // Hanya ambil yang pertama
        }

        // 8. Cleanup
        pSvc->Release();
        pLoc->Release();
        pEnumerator->Release();
        CoUninitialize();

        return result;
    }

    std::string HWIDGenerator::HashString(const std::string& input) {
        // Kode hashing sama seperti di FileIntegrityChecker
        BCRYPT_ALG_HANDLE hAlg = NULL;
        BCRYPT_HASH_HANDLE hHash = NULL;
        NTSTATUS status = STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
        DWORD cbHash = 0, cbHashObject = 0;
        PBYTE pbHashObject = NULL;
        PBYTE pbHash = NULL;

        status = BCryptOpenAlgorithmProvider(&hAlg, BCRYPT_SHA256_ALGORITHM, NULL, 0);
        if (!BCRYPT_SUCCESS(status)) { return ""; }
        DWORD cbData = 0;
        status = BCryptGetProperty(hAlg, BCRYPT_OBJECT_LENGTH, (PBYTE)&cbHashObject, sizeof(DWORD), &cbData, 0);
        if (!BCRYPT_SUCCESS(status)) { BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0); return ""; }
        status = BCryptGetProperty(hAlg, BCRYPT_HASH_LENGTH, (PBYTE)&cbHash, sizeof(DWORD), &cbData, 0);
        if (!BCRYPT_SUCCESS(status)) { BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0); return ""; }

        pbHashObject = (PBYTE)HeapAlloc(GetProcessHeap(), 0, cbHashObject);
        pbHash = (PBYTE)HeapAlloc(GetProcessHeap(), 0, cbHash);
        if (!pbHashObject || !pbHash) { HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHashObject); HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHash); BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0); return ""; }

        status = BCryptCreateHash(hAlg, &hHash, pbHashObject, cbHashObject, NULL, 0, 0);
        if (!BCRYPT_SUCCESS(status)) { HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHashObject); HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHash); BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0); return ""; }
        status = BCryptHashData(hHash, (PBYTE)input.c_str(), (ULONG)input.length(), 0);
        if (!BCRYPT_SUCCESS(status)) { BCryptDestroyHash(hHash); HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHashObject); HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHash); BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0); return ""; }
        status = BCryptFinishHash(hHash, pbHash, cbHash, 0);
        if (!BCRYPT_SUCCESS(status)) { BCryptDestroyHash(hHash); HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHashObject); HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHash); BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0); return ""; }

        std::stringstream ss;
        ss << std::hex << std::setfill('0');
        for (DWORD i = 0; i < cbHash; i++) { ss << std::setw(2) << static_cast<unsigned int>(pbHash[i]); }

        BCryptDestroyHash(hHash);
        HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHashObject);
        HeapFree(GetProcessHeap(), 0, pbHash);
        BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(hAlg, 0);
        return ss.str();
    }
}
```

`client/src/iat_hook_scanner.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "iat_hook_scanner.h"
#include <iostream>

namespace Oblivion {
    IATHookScanner::IATHookScanner() {}

    IATHookScanner::~IATHookScanner() {}

    void IATHookScanner::PerformScan() {
        // Dapatkan base address dari modul utama (rro.exe)
        HMODULE hMod = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
        if (!hMod) {
            return;
        }

        // Konversi ke byte pointer untuk perhitungan offset
        BYTE* pBase = (BYTE*)hMod;

        // Dapatkan DOS Header
        PIMAGE_DOS_HEADER pDosHeader = (PIMAGE_DOS_HEADER)pBase;
        if (pDosHeader->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) {
            return; // Bukan PE yang valid
        }

        // Dapatkan NT Headers
        PIMAGE_NT_HEADERS pNtHeaders = (PIMAGE_NT_HEADERS)(pBase + pDosHeader->e_lfanew);
        if (pNtHeaders->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) {
            return; // Bukan PE yang valid
        }

        // Dapatkan Import Descriptor
        PIMAGE_IMPORT_DESCRIPTOR pImportDir = (PIMAGE_IMPORT_DESCRIPTOR)(pBase + pNtHeaders->OptionalHeader.DataDirectory[IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT].VirtualAddress);
        if (!pImportDir) {
            return; // Tidak ada tabel impor
        }

        // Iterasi melalui semua DLL yang diimpor
        while (pImportDir->Characteristics && pImportDir->Name) {
            // Dapatkan nama DLL yang diimpor
            char* szDllName = (char*)(pBase + pImportDir->Name);
            HMODULE hImportedDll = GetModuleHandleA(szDllName);
            if (!hImportedDll) {
                pImportDir++;
                continue; // Lanjut jika DLL tidak dimuat (seharusnya tidak terjadi)
            }

            // Dapatkan pointer ke OriginalFirstThunk (berisi nama fungsi) dan FirstThunk (IAT)
            PIMAGE_THUNK_DATA pOrigThunk = (PIMAGE_THUNK_DATA)(pBase + pImportDir->OriginalFirstThunk);
            PIMAGE_THUNK_DATA pThunk = (PIMAGE_THUNK_DATA)(pBase + pImportDir->FirstThunk);

            // Iterasi melalui semua fungsi yang diimpor dari DLL ini
            while (pOrigThunk->u1.AddressOfData && pThunk->u1.Function) {
                // Kita hanya memeriksa fungsi yang diimpor dengan nama, bukan dengan ordinal
                if (!(pOrigThunk->u1.Ordinal & IMAGE_ORDINAL_FLAG)) {
                    PIMAGE_IMPORT_BY_NAME pImportName = (PIMAGE_IMPORT_BY_NAME)(pBase + pOrigThunk->u1.AddressOfData);
                    char* szFuncName = pImportName->Name;

                    // Dapatkan alamat asli fungsi menggunakan GetProcAddress
                    FARPROC pRealAddr = GetProcAddress(hImportedDll, szFuncName);
                    if (!pRealAddr) {
                        pOrigThunk++;
                        pThunk++;
                        continue; // Fungsi tidak ditemukan, lanjut
                    }

                    // Dapatkan al fungsi dari IAT
                    FARPROC pIatAddr = (FARPROC)pThunk->u1.Function;

                    // BANDINGKAN! Jika tidak cocok, berarti ada HOOK.
                    if (pRealAddr != pIatAddr) {
                        std::wstring message = L"IAT Hook terdeteksi!\n\nFungsi: ";
                        message += std::wstring(szFuncName, szFuncName + strlen(szFuncName));
                        message += L"\nDLL: ";
                        message += std::wstring(szDllName, szDllName + strlen(szDllName));
                        message += L"\n\nClient akan ditutup.";
                        MessageBoxW(NULL, message.c_str(), L"Oblivion Hack Shield - Violation", MB_OK | MB_ICONERROR | MB_TOPMOST);
                        ExitProcess(0);
                    }
                }
                pOrigThunk++;
                pThunk++;
            }
            pImportDir++;
        }
    }
}
```

`client/src/injection_scanner.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "injection_scanner.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <tlhelp32.h>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cwctype> // Untuk towlower

// Forward declaration
extern void ReportViolationAndExit(const std::wstring& message, const std::string& type, const std::string& details);

namespace Oblivion {
    InjectionScanner::InjectionScanner() {}

    InjectionScanner::~InjectionScanner() {}

    void InjectionScanner::PerformScan() {
        // Ambil snapshot dari semua modul yang dimuat oleh proses saat ini
        HANDLE hSnapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPMODULE | TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, GetCurrentProcessId());
        if (hSnapshot == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
            // Gagal mengambil snapshot, log error jika perlu
            return;
        }

        MODULEENTRY32W me32;
        me32.dwSize = sizeof(MODULEENTRY32W);

        // Dapatkan informasi modul pertama
        if (Module32FirstW(hSnapshot, &me32)) {
            do {
                // Periksa apakah modul (DLL) ini ada di daftar hitam
                if (IsDllBlacklisted(me32.szModule)) {
                    // Jika iya, hentikan client
                    // Konversi DLL name ke UTF-8 untuk details
                    std::string details_utf8;
                    int size_needed = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, me32.szModule, -1, NULL, 0, NULL, NULL);
                    if (size_needed > 0) {
                        details_utf8.assign(size_needed - 1, 0); // -1 untuk menghilangkan null terminator
                        WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, me32.szModule, -1, &details_utf8[0], size_needed, NULL, NULL);
                    }
                    
                    // Panggil fungsi pelaporan global
                    ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "INJECTION_DETECTED", details_utf8);
                }

            } while (Module32NextW(hSnapshot, &me32));
        }

        // Tutup handle snapshot
        CloseHandle(hSnapshot);
    }

    bool InjectionScanner::IsDllBlacklisted(const wchar_t* dllName) {
        // Daftar nama DLL yang dicurigai.
        // PERHATIAN: Hati-hati menambahkan daftar ini untuk menghindari false positive.
        // Fokus pada DLL yang spesifik untuk cheat.
        static const std::vector<std::wstring> blacklistedDlls = {
            L"wexdist.dll", // Sering digunakan oleh OpenKore
            L"macro.dll",
            L"rpehook.dll",
            L"wpehook.dll",
            L"cheat.dll",
            L"injector.dll",
            L"autopot.dll",
            L"bot.dll"
        };

        std::wstring moduleName = dllName;
        // Ubah ke lowercase untuk perbandingan case-insensitive
        std::transform(moduleName.begin(), moduleName.end(), moduleName.begin(), ::towlower);

        for (const auto& blacklistedName : blacklistedDlls) {
            if (moduleName == blacklistedName) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }
}
```

```cpp
// Forward declaration

// CRITICAL: Baseline dibuat dari DISK, bukan dari memori yang mungkin sudah terinfeksi
// Multi-layer verification:
// 1. PE structure validation
// 2. Embedded signature verification (build-time)
// 3. Disk-based baseline creation
// 4. Cross-verification (disk vs memory)
// 5. Runtime integrity monitoring

namespace Oblivion {
    MemoryScanner::MemoryScanner() 
        : m_baselineCreated(false)
        , m_baselineVerified(false)
        , m_moduleBaseAddress(0)

    // === Cryptographic Hash Functions ===
    
    uint32_t MemoryScanner::CalculateCRC32(const uint8_t* data, size_t size) {
        // Tabel CRC32 lookup (polynomial 0xEDB88320)
        static const uint32_t crc32_table[256] = {
            0x00000000, 0x77073096, 0xEE0E612C, 0x990951BA, 0x076DC419, 0x706AF48F,

            0xA4D1C46D, 0xD3D6F4FB, 0x4369E96A, 0x346ED9FC, 0xAD678846, 0xDA60B8D0,
            0x44042D73, 0x33031DE5, 0xAA0A4C5F, 0xDD0D7CC9, 0x5005713C, 0x270241AA,
```
