`client/src/process_watcher.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "process_watcher.h"
#include "blacklist.h"
#include "AntiDebug.h"
#include "injection_scanner.h"
#include "anti_suspend.h"
#include "overlay_scanner.h"
#include "memory_scanner.h"
#include "digital_signature_validator.h"
#include "iat_hook_scanner.h"
#include "advanced_process_heuristics.h"
#include <tlhelp32.h>
#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <random>
#include <chrono>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <ctime>

// Forward declaration
extern void ReportViolationAndExit(const std::wstring& message, const std::string& type, const std::string& details);

namespace Oblivion {
    ProcessWatcher::ProcessWatcher() : m_isRunning(false) {
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Initializing components..." << std::endl;
        try {
            m_pAntiDebug = new AntiDebug();
            m_pInjectionScanner = new InjectionScanner();
            m_pAntiSuspend = new AntiSuspend();
            m_pOverlayScanner = new OverlayScanner();
            m_pMemoryScanner = new MemoryScanner();
            m_pDigitalSignatureValidator = new DigitalSignatureValidator();
            m_pIATHookScanner = new IATHookScanner();
            m_pAdvancedHeuristics = new AdvancedProcessHeuristics();
            
            // Set heartbeat callback untuk AdvancedHeuristics
            m_pAdvancedHeuristics->SetHeartbeatCallback([this]() {
                if (m_isRunning && m_pAntiSuspend) {
                    m_pAntiSuspend->UpdateHeartbeat();
                }
            });
            
            std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] All components initialized successfully" << std::endl;
        } catch (const std::exception& e) {
            std::cerr << "[ProcessWatcher] ERROR: Failed to initialize: " << e.what() << std::endl;
            throw;
        }
    }

    ProcessWatcher::~ProcessWatcher() {
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Destructor called, stopping..." << std::endl;
        Stop();
        
        // Delete all components safely
        if (m_pAntiDebug) { delete m_pAntiDebug; m_pAntiDebug = nullptr; }
        if (m_pInjectionScanner) { delete m_pInjectionScanner; m_pInjectionScanner = nullptr; }
        if (m_pAntiSuspend) { delete m_pAntiSuspend; m_pAntiSuspend = nullptr; }
        if (m_pOverlayScanner) { delete m_pOverlayScanner; m_pOverlayScanner = nullptr; }
        if (m_pMemoryScanner) { delete m_pMemoryScanner; m_pMemoryScanner = nullptr; }
        if (m_pDigitalSignatureValidator) { delete m_pDigitalSignatureValidator; m_pDigitalSignatureValidator = nullptr; }
        if (m_pIATHookScanner) { delete m_pIATHookScanner; m_pIATHookScanner = nullptr; }
        if (m_pAdvancedHeuristics) { delete m_pAdvancedHeuristics; m_pAdvancedHeuristics = nullptr; }
        
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Destructor complete" << std::endl;
    }

    void ProcessWatcher::Start() {
        if (m_isRunning) {
            std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Already running, ignoring Start() call" << std::endl;
            return;
        }
        
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Starting monitoring system..." << std::endl;
        m_isRunning = true;
        
        // Mulai thread pengawas terlebih dahulu
        m_pAntiSuspend->Start();
        
        // Lalu mulai thread pemantauan utama
        m_watcherThread = std::thread(&ProcessWatcher::WatcherLoop, this);
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Monitoring thread started" << std::endl;
    }

    void ProcessWatcher::Stop() {
        if (!m_isRunning) {
            return;
        }
        
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Stopping monitoring system..." << std::endl;
        m_isRunning = false;
        
        // Wait for watcher thread to finish
        if (m_watcherThread.joinable()) {
            m_watcherThread.join();
        }
        
        // Hentikan thread pengawas setelah thread utama berhenti
        m_pAntiSuspend->Stop();
        std::cout << "[ProcessWatcher] Stopped successfully" << std::endl;
    }

    void ProcessWatcher::WatcherLoop() {
        auto heartbeat = [this]() {
            if (m_isRunning) {
                m_pAntiSuspend->UpdateHeartbeat();
            }
        };

        heartbeat();
        // Pemindaian awal
        PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pAntiDebug->PerformCheck();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pInjectionScanner->PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pOverlayScanner->PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pMemoryScanner->PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pDigitalSignatureValidator->PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pIATHookScanner->PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        if (m_isRunning) m_pAdvancedHeuristics->PerformScan();
        heartbeat();

        // Gunakan generator angka acak modern untuk interval tidur acak
        std::mt19937 rng(static_cast<unsigned int>(
            std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now().time_since_epoch().count()));
        std::uniform_int_distribution<int> sleepDist(500, 3000);

        // Loop pemantauan real-time
        while (m_isRunning) {
            heartbeat();

            PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pAntiDebug->PerformCheck();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pInjectionScanner->PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pOverlayScanner->PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pMemoryScanner->PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pDigitalSignatureValidator->PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pIATHookScanner->PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) m_pAdvancedHeuristics->PerformScan();

            if (m_isRunning) {
                const int sleepDurationMs = sleepDist(rng);

#ifdef _DEBUG
                // Log waktu eksekusi loop untuk verifikasi interval acak.
                auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
                auto tt = std::chrono::system_clock::to_time_t(now);
                auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(now.time_since_epoch()) % 1000;
                std::tm localTime{};
                localtime_s(&localTime, &tt);

                std::ostringstream oss;
                oss << "[ProcessWatcher] Scan loop at "
                    << std::put_time(&localTime, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                    << '.' << std::setw(3) << std::setfill('0') << ms.count()
                    << " sleeping " << sleepDurationMs << " ms";

                const auto message = oss.str();
                OutputDebugStringA(message.c_str());
                OutputDebugStringA("\n");
#endif

                Sleep(sleepDurationMs);
            }
        }
    }

    void ProcessWatcher::PerformScan() {
        HANDLE hSnapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
        if (hSnapshot == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
            return;
        }

        PROCESSENTRY32 pe32;
        pe32.dwSize = sizeof(PROCESSENTRY32);

        if (Process32First(hSnapshot, &pe32)) {
            do {
                std::wstring processName = pe32.szExeFile;
                DWORD processId = pe32.th32ProcessID;

                // Deteksi blacklist
                if (IsProcessBlacklisted(processName)) {
                    std::wcout << L"[DETECTION] Blacklisted process detected: " << processName << std::endl;
                    m_isRunning = false;
                    std::string details_utf8;
                    int size_needed = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, &processName[0], (int)processName.size(), NULL, 0, NULL, NULL);
                    if (size_needed > 0) {
                        details_utf8.assign(size_needed, 0);
                        WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, &processName[0], (int)processName.size(), &details_utf8[0], size_needed, NULL, NULL);
                    }
                    std::cout << "[VIOLATION] Reporting to server: PROCESS_DETECTED - " << details_utf8 << std::endl;
                    ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "PROCESS_DETECTED", details_utf8);
                }

                // Cek apakah proses sudah pernah di-scan
                bool isNewProcess = m_scannedProcesses.find(processId) == m_scannedProcesses.end();
                
                if (isNewProcess) {
                    // Tambahkan ke cache
                    m_scannedProcesses.insert(processId);
                    
                    // Memory scan untuk proses non-whitelist
                    if (Oblivion::ShouldPerformMemoryScan(processName)) {
                        std::wcout << L"[SCAN] Memory scan scheduled for: " << processName << L" (PID: " << processId << L")" << std::endl;
                    }

                    // Heuristik perilaku
                    if (Oblivion::IsSuspiciousBehavior(processId)) {
                        Oblivion::LogSuspiciousProcess(processName, processId, "Suspicious module or injection detected");
                        // Optional: langsung blokir
                        std::string details_utf8;
                        int size_needed = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, &processName[0], (int)processName.size(), NULL, 0, NULL, NULL);
                        if (size_needed > 0) {
                            details_utf8.assign(size_needed, 0);
                            WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, &processName[0], (int)processName.size(), &details_utf8[0], size_needed, NULL, NULL);
                        }
                        ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "SUSPICIOUS_BEHAVIOR", details_utf8);
                    }
                }

            } while (Process32Next(hSnapshot, &pe32) && m_isRunning);
        }

        CloseHandle(hSnapshot);
    }
}

`client/src/secret_obf.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "secret_obf.h"
#include <vector>
#include <windows.h>

// Obfuscation ringan untuk shared secret. Hindari menyimpan literal string.

namespace Oblivion {
    std::string GetClientSecret() {
        static std::string cached;
        if (!cached.empty()) {
            return cached;
        }

        // 1) Coba ambil dari environment variable (runtime configurable)
        //    Set dengan nama OBLIVION_SECRET untuk override tanpa recompile
        wchar_t envBuf[256] = {0};
        DWORD envLen = GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"OBLIVION_SECRET", envBuf, 255);
        if (envLen > 0 && envLen < 255) {
            int size_needed = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, envBuf, (int)envLen, NULL, 0, NULL, NULL);
            if (size_needed > 0) {
                std::string envSecret(size_needed, '\0');
                WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, envBuf, (int)envLen, &envSecret[0], size_needed, NULL, NULL);
                cached = envSecret;
                return cached;
            }
        }

        // 2) Coba load dari file berdampingan (secret.dat) untuk produksi
        //    File berisi bytes yang sudah di-XOR (encoded) agar tidak terlihat literal
        std::wstring exePathW;
        wchar_t pathBuf[MAX_PATH] = {0};
        DWORD sz = GetModuleFileNameW(NULL, pathBuf, MAX_PATH);
        if (sz > 0) {
            exePathW.assign(pathBuf, sz);
            size_t lastSlash = exePathW.find_last_of(L"\\/");
            if (lastSlash != std::wstring::npos) exePathW = exePathW.substr(0, lastSlash + 1);
        }
        std::wstring secretPathW = exePathW + L"secret.dat";
        HANDLE hFile = CreateFileW(secretPathW.c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, NULL, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, NULL);
        if (hFile != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
            DWORD fileSize = GetFileSize(hFile, NULL);
            if (fileSize > 0 && fileSize < 1024) {
                std::vector<unsigned char> encoded(fileSize);
                DWORD readBytes = 0;
                if (ReadFile(hFile, encoded.data(), fileSize, &readBytes, NULL) && readBytes == fileSize) {
                    std::string secret;
                    secret.reserve(fileSize);
                    for (size_t i = 0; i < encoded.size(); ++i) {
                        unsigned char key = (unsigned char)(0x5A + ((i * 7) % 0x3F));
                        secret += (char)(encoded[i] ^ key);
                    }
                    CloseHandle(hFile);
                    cached = secret;
                    return cached;
                }
            }
            CloseHandle(hFile);
        }

        // 3) Fallback development: gunakan nilai dummy agar terlihat jelas di log
        //    Disarankan untuk produksi set via ENV atau file secret.dat
        const char* devDefault = "DEV_SECRET_NOT_SET";
        size_t len = strlen(devDefault);
        std::vector<unsigned char> encoded(len);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
            unsigned char key = (unsigned char)(0x5A + ((i * 7) % 0x3F));
            encoded[i] = (unsigned char)(devDefault[i] ^ key);
        }
        std::string secret;
        secret.reserve(len);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
            unsigned char key = (unsigned char)(0x5A + ((i * 7) % 0x3F));
            secret += (char)(encoded[i] ^ key);
        }

        cached = secret;
        return cached;
    }
}

`client/src/self_integrity_guard.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pch.h"
#include "self_integrity_guard.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <fstream>

// Forward declaration
extern void ReportViolationAndExit(const std::wstring& message, const std::string& type, const std::string& details);

namespace {
    constexpr auto kVerificationInterval = std::chrono::seconds(5);

    bool IsCodeSection(const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER& section) {
        const auto characteristics = section.Characteristics;
        return (characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_CNT_CODE) != 0 &&
               (characteristics & IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE) != 0;
    }
}

namespace Oblivion {
    SelfIntegrityGuard::SelfIntegrityGuard(HMODULE moduleHandle)
        : m_module(moduleHandle),
          m_running(false) {
        if (m_module) {
            wchar_t pathBuffer[MAX_PATH] = { 0 };
            if (GetModuleFileNameW(m_module, pathBuffer, MAX_PATH) != 0) {
                m_modulePath.assign(pathBuffer);
            }
        }
    }

    SelfIntegrityGuard::~SelfIntegrityGuard() {
        Stop();
    }

    bool SelfIntegrityGuard::Start() {
        if (m_running.load() || !m_module || m_modulePath.empty()) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!LoadDiskImage()) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!ParseRelocations()) {
            return false;
        }

        if (!VerifySectionsOnce()) {
            return false;
        }

        m_running = true;
        m_worker = std::thread(&SelfIntegrityGuard::MonitoringLoop, this);
        return true;
    }

    void SelfIntegrityGuard::Stop() {
        if (!m_running.load()) {
            return;
        }

        m_running = false;
        if (m_worker.joinable()) {
            m_worker.join();
        }
    }

    bool SelfIntegrityGuard::LoadDiskImage() {
        if (!m_diskImage.empty()) {
            return true;
        }

        std::ifstream file(m_modulePath, std::ios::binary | std::ios::ate);
        if (!file.is_open()) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto fileSize = file.tellg();
        if (fileSize <= 0) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto sizeValue = static_cast<std::streamsize>(fileSize);
        m_diskImage.resize(static_cast<size_t>(sizeValue));
        file.seekg(0, std::ios::beg);
        file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(m_diskImage.data()), sizeValue);
        return file.good();
    }

    void SelfIntegrityGuard::MonitoringLoop() {
        while (m_running.load()) {
            if (!VerifySectionsOnce()) {
                // ReportViolationAndExit already called inside VerifySectionsOnce.
                return;
            }

            auto slept = std::chrono::milliseconds(0);
            while (slept < kVerificationInterval && m_running.load()) {
                Sleep(100);
                slept += std::chrono::milliseconds(100);
            }
        }
    }

    bool SelfIntegrityGuard::VerifySectionsOnce() const {
        auto* moduleBase = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(m_module);
        const auto* dosHeader = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(moduleBase);
        if (!dosHeader || dosHeader->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* ntHeaders = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(moduleBase + dosHeader->e_lfanew);
        if (!ntHeaders || ntHeaders->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* fileDosHeader = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(m_diskImage.data());
        if (!fileDosHeader || fileDosHeader->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* fileNtHeaders = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(m_diskImage.data() + fileDosHeader->e_lfanew);
        if (!fileNtHeaders || fileNtHeaders->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto sectionCount = ntHeaders->FileHeader.NumberOfSections;
        auto* currentSection = IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(ntHeaders);

        for (WORD i = 0; i < sectionCount; ++i, ++currentSection) {
            if (!IsCodeSection(*currentSection)) {
                continue;
            }

            if (!CompareSection(*currentSection)) {
                size_t length = 0;
                while (length < IMAGE_SIZEOF_SHORT_NAME && currentSection->Name[length] != '\0') {
                    ++length;
                }
                const std::string sectionName(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(currentSection->Name), length);
                ReportTamper(sectionName);
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    bool SelfIntegrityGuard::CompareSection(const IMAGE_SECTION_HEADER& section) const {
        auto* moduleBase = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(m_module);
        const auto* sectionMemory = moduleBase + section.VirtualAddress;

        size_t sectionSize = static_cast<size_t>(section.SizeOfRawData);
        const size_t virtualSize = static_cast<size_t>(section.Misc.VirtualSize);
        if (virtualSize > 0 && virtualSize < sectionSize) {
            sectionSize = virtualSize;
        }

        if (sectionSize == 0) {
            return true;
        }

        if (section.PointerToRawData + sectionSize > m_diskImage.size()) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* sectionOnDisk = m_diskImage.data() + section.PointerToRawData;

        std::vector<std::pair<size_t, size_t>> skipIntervals;
        const DWORD sectionStartRva = section.VirtualAddress;
        const DWORD sectionEndRva = sectionStartRva + static_cast<DWORD>(sectionSize);
        BuildSkipIntervals(sectionStartRva, sectionEndRva, skipIntervals);

        if (skipIntervals.empty()) {
            return memcmp(sectionMemory, sectionOnDisk, sectionSize) == 0;
        }

        size_t currentOffset = 0;
        for (const auto& interval : skipIntervals) {
            const size_t start = interval.first;
            const size_t end = interval.second;
            if (currentOffset < start) {
                const size_t length = start - currentOffset;
                if (memcmp(sectionMemory + currentOffset, sectionOnDisk + currentOffset, length) != 0) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            currentOffset = std::max(currentOffset, end);
        }

        if (currentOffset < sectionSize) {
            const size_t length = sectionSize - currentOffset;
            if (memcmp(sectionMemory + currentOffset, sectionOnDisk + currentOffset, length) != 0) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    void SelfIntegrityGuard::ReportTamper(const std::string& sectionName) const {
        std::string details = "Self integrity mismatch detected";
        if (!sectionName.empty()) {
            details += " in section ";
            details += sectionName;
        }
        ReportViolationAndExit(L"", "SELF_TAMPER_DETECTED", details);
    }

    bool SelfIntegrityGuard::ParseRelocations() {
        m_relocationSpans.clear();

        if (m_diskImage.empty()) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* dosHeader = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(m_diskImage.data());
        if (!dosHeader || dosHeader->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* ntHeaders = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(m_diskImage.data() + dosHeader->e_lfanew);
        if (!ntHeaders || ntHeaders->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto& relocDirectory = ntHeaders->OptionalHeader.DataDirectory[IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_BASERELOC];
        if (relocDirectory.VirtualAddress == 0 || relocDirectory.Size == 0) {
            return true;
        }

        DWORD relocFileOffset = 0;
        if (!RvaToFileRange(relocDirectory.VirtualAddress, relocDirectory.Size, relocFileOffset)) {
            return false;
        }

        size_t remaining = relocDirectory.Size;
        const uint8_t* cursor = m_diskImage.data() + relocFileOffset;

        while (remaining >= sizeof(IMAGE_BASE_RELOCATION)) {
            const auto* block = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_BASE_RELOCATION*>(cursor);
            if (block->SizeOfBlock < sizeof(IMAGE_BASE_RELOCATION) || block->SizeOfBlock > remaining) {
                break;
            }

            const size_t entryCount = (block->SizeOfBlock - sizeof(IMAGE_BASE_RELOCATION)) / sizeof(WORD);
            const auto* entry = reinterpret_cast<const WORD*>(cursor + sizeof(IMAGE_BASE_RELOCATION));

            for (size_t i = 0; i < entryCount; ++i) {
                const WORD typeOffset = entry[i];
                const WORD type = typeOffset >> 12;
                const WORD offset = typeOffset & 0x0FFF;

                size_t spanSize = 0;
                switch (type) {
                case IMAGE_REL_BASED_ABSOLUTE:
                    continue;
                case IMAGE_REL_BASED_HIGHLOW:
                    spanSize = sizeof(DWORD);
                    break;
                case IMAGE_REL_BASED_DIR64:
                    spanSize = sizeof(ULONGLONG);
                    break;
                default:
                    continue;
                }

                const DWORD rva = block->VirtualAddress + offset;
                m_relocationSpans.push_back({ rva, spanSize });
            }

            cursor += block->SizeOfBlock;
            remaining -= block->SizeOfBlock;
        }

        std::sort(m_relocationSpans.begin(), m_relocationSpans.end(), [](const RelocationSpan& a, const RelocationSpan& b) {
            return a.rva < b.rva;
            });

        return true;
    }

    bool SelfIntegrityGuard::RvaToFileRange(DWORD rva, DWORD size, DWORD& fileOffset) const {
        if (m_diskImage.empty()) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* dosHeader = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(m_diskImage.data());
        if (!dosHeader || dosHeader->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* ntHeaders = reinterpret_cast<const IMAGE_NT_HEADERS*>(m_diskImage.data() + dosHeader->e_lfanew);
        if (!ntHeaders || ntHeaders->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) {
            return false;
        }

        const DWORD imageSize = ntHeaders->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage;
        if (size == 0 || rva >= imageSize) {
            return false;
        }

        const auto* section = IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(ntHeaders);
        for (WORD i = 0; i < ntHeaders->FileHeader.NumberOfSections; ++i, ++section) {
            const DWORD sectionStart = section->VirtualAddress;
            const DWORD sectionSize = std::max(section->Misc.VirtualSize, section->SizeOfRawData);
            const DWORD sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

            if (rva >= sectionStart && rva < sectionEnd) {
                const DWORD offsetWithinSection = rva - sectionStart;
                if (offsetWithinSection + size > sectionSize) {
                    return false;
                }

                fileOffset = section->PointerToRawData + offsetWithinSection;
                if (fileOffset + size > m_diskImage.size()) {
                    return false;
                }
                return true;
            }
        }

        // RVA within headers
        if (rva < ntHeaders->OptionalHeader.SizeOfHeaders) {
            fileOffset = rva;
            if (fileOffset + size > m_diskImage.size()) {
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    void SelfIntegrityGuard::BuildSkipIntervals(DWORD sectionStart, DWORD sectionEnd, std::vector<std::pair<size_t, size_t>>& skips) const {
        skips.clear();
        if (m_relocationSpans.empty() || sectionStart >= sectionEnd) {
            return;
        }

        for (const auto& span : m_relocationSpans) {
            const DWORD spanStart = span.rva;
            const DWORD spanEnd = span.rva + static_cast<DWORD>(span.size);

            if (spanEnd <= sectionStart || spanStart >= sectionEnd) {
                continue;
            }

            const auto relativeStart = static_cast<size_t>(std::max<DWORD>(spanStart, sectionStart) - sectionStart);
            const auto relativeEnd = static_cast<size_t>(std::min<DWORD>(spanEnd, sectionEnd) - sectionStart);
            if (relativeStart < relativeEnd) {
                skips.emplace_back(relativeStart, relativeEnd);
            }
        }

        if (skips.empty()) {
            return;
        }

        std::sort(skips.begin(), skips.end());

        std::vector<std::pair<size_t, size_t>> merged;
        merged.reserve(skips.size());

        auto current = skips.front();
        for (size_t i = 1; i < skips.size(); ++i) {
            const auto& interval = skips[i];
            if (interval.first <= current.second) {
                current.second = std::max(current.second, interval.second);
            } else {
                merged.push_back(current);
                current = interval;
            }
        }
        merged.push_back(current);

        skips.swap(merged);
    }
}


```

`server/Makefile`:

```
# Makefile for Oblivion Server (Linux/Debian)
# Usage: make
#        make clean
#        make install (optional)

CXX = g++
CXXFLAGS = -std=c++17 -Wall -Wextra -O2 -pthread
LDFLAGS = -lssl -lcrypto -lpthread

# Directories
SRC_DIR = src
INCLUDE_DIR = include
BUILD_DIR = build

# Source files
SOURCES = server_linux.cpp $(SRC_DIR)/secret_obf.cpp
OBJECTS = $(SOURCES:.cpp=.o)
TARGET = oblivion-server

# Default target
all: $(TARGET)

# Build target
$(TARGET): $(OBJECTS)
	$(CXX) $(CXXFLAGS) -o $(TARGET) $(OBJECTS) $(LDFLAGS)
	@echo "Build complete: $(TARGET)"

# Compile source files
%.o: %.cpp
	$(CXX) $(CXXFLAGS) -I$(INCLUDE_DIR) -c $< -o $@

# Clean build artifacts
clean:
	rm -f $(OBJECTS) $(TARGET)
	@echo "Clean complete"

# Install (optional - copies to /usr/local/bin)
install: $(TARGET)
	cp $(TARGET) /usr/local/bin/
	@echo "Installed to /usr/local/bin/$(TARGET)"

# Uninstall
uninstall:
	rm -f /usr/local/bin/$(TARGET)
	@echo "Uninstalled from /usr/local/bin/$(TARGET)"

# Phony targets
.PHONY: all clean install uninstall


```

`server/README_LINUX.md`:

```md
# Oblivion Server - Linux Build Guide

## Requirements

### Debian/Ubuntu
```bash
sudo apt-get update
sudo apt-get install -y build-essential libssl-dev
```

### Dependencies
- g++ (C++17 compiler)
- OpenSSL development libraries (libssl-dev)
- pthread (usually included with g++)

## Build Instructions

### 1. Navigate to server directory
```bash
cd server
```

### 2. Build the server
```bash
make
```

### 3. Make management script executable and fix line endings
```bash
# Install dos2unix (if not already installed)
sudo apt-get install -y dos2unix

# Fix Windows line endings (if transferred from Windows)
dos2unix oblivion-start

# Make executable
chmod +x oblivion-start

# Test the script
./oblivion-start
```

**Note:** If you get "bad interpreter" error, it means the file has Windows line endings (CRLF). Run `dos2unix oblivion-start` to fix it.

### 4. Run the server

**Option A: Using management script (recommended)**
```bash
# Interactive mode (shows usage and prompts for input)
./oblivion-start

# Or with command directly
./oblivion-start start
```

**Option B: Run directly**
```bash
./oblivion-server
```

## Configuration

### Environment Variables
You can configure heartbeat settings via environment variables:

```bash
export OBLV_HB_INTERVAL_MS=3000
export OBLV_HB_JITTER_MS=250
export OBLV_HB_TIMEOUT_MS=4000
export OBLV_HB_MAX_MISS=3
export OBLV_HB_RTT_WARN_MS=1500
./server
```

### Server Secret
The server requires a secret key. You can set it via:

1. **Environment variable** (recommended):
   ```bash
   export OBLIVION_SECRET="your-secret-key-here"
   ./server
   ```

2. **File-based** (secret.dat):
   - Create a file named `secret.dat` in the same directory as the server executable
   - The file should contain XOR-encoded secret (same format as Windows version)

## Files

- `bans.txt` - Ban list (one HWID per line, comments with #)
- `violations.log` - Log of all violations detected
- `secret.dat` - Optional: XOR-encoded server secret

## Server Management Script

The `oblivion-start` script provides easy server management with interactive mode:

### Interactive Mode
```bash
# Run without arguments to enter interactive mode
./oblivion-start
# Output:
# Usage: ./oblivion-start { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }
# Enter a valid option: 
```

### Direct Commands
```bash
# Start server
./oblivion-start start

# Stop server
./oblivion-start stop

# Restart server
./oblivion-start restart

# Check status
./oblivion-start status

# View log (last 50 lines)
./oblivion-start log

# View live log (follow mode)
./oblivion-start watch
# or
./oblivion-start log tail

# Show help
./oblivion-start help
```

### Status Output
The status command shows:
- Server running status (RUNNING/STOPPED)
- Process ID (PID)
- Port listening status
- Recent log entries
- Process information

## Running as a Service

### Using systemd (recommended)

Create `/etc/systemd/system/oblivion-server.service`:

```ini
[Unit]
Description=Oblivion Hack Shield Server
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
User=your-user
WorkingDirectory=/path/to/server
ExecStart=/path/to/server/server
Restart=always
RestartSec=10
Environment="OBLIVION_SECRET=your-secret-key"

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

Then:
```bash
sudo systemctl daemon-reload
sudo systemctl enable oblivion-server
sudo systemctl start oblivion-server
sudo systemctl status oblivion-server
```

## Troubleshooting

### Port already in use
If port 54321 is already in use, you can change it by modifying `DEFAULT_PORT` in `server_linux.cpp` or use a port forwarder.

### Permission denied
Make sure the server executable has execute permissions:
```bash
chmod +x server
```

### SSL library not found
Install OpenSSL development package:
```bash
sudo apt-get install libssl-dev
```

## Clean Build

To clean build artifacts:
```bash
make clean
```

## Notes

- The server listens on port 54321 by default
- All logs and ban files are created in the same directory as the executable
- The server supports multiple concurrent client connections
- Heartbeat monitoring is enabled by default


```

`server/fix-line-endings.sh`:

```sh
#!/bin/bash
# Fix line endings for oblivion-start script
# Run this once: bash fix-line-endings.sh

if [ -f "oblivion-start" ]; then
    # Remove carriage returns (^M)
    sed -i 's/\r$//' oblivion-start
    chmod +x oblivion-start
    echo "Fixed line endings for oblivion-start"
else
    echo "Error: oblivion-start not found"
    exit 1
fi

`server/include/heartbeat_protocol.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

namespace Oblivion {
    enum class HbType : uint8_t { Ping = 1, Pong = 2 };

    struct ClientStatus {
        bool antiSuspendOk;
        bool integrityOk;
    };

    struct HeartbeatMessage {
        HbType type;
        uint32_t seq;
        uint64_t t_send_ms;
        uint64_t nonce;
        ClientStatus status;    // ignored for Ping
        std::string hmac;       // hex HMAC-SHA256
    };

    struct HeartbeatConfig {
        uint32_t interval_ms = 3000;
        uint32_t jitter_ms = 250;
        uint32_t timeout_ms = 4000;
        uint32_t max_consecutive_miss = 3;
        uint32_t rtt_warn_ms = 1500;
    };
}

```

#include <string>

// Obfuscation ringan server-side untuk menyembunyikan secret dari string literal.
// Catatan: Hanya anti-string-scanning, bukan proteksi kriptografi.
namespace OblivionServer {
    std::string GetServerSecret();
}

`server/oblivion-server-start`:

```
#!/bin/bash
# Oblivion Server Management Script
# Usage: ./oblivion-server-start {start|stop|restart|status|reload|log}

# Configuration
SERVER_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
SERVER_BIN="$SERVER_DIR/oblivion-server"
PID_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/oblivion-server.pid"
LOG_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/server.log"
LOCK_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/oblivion-server.lock"

# Colors for output
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

# Check if server binary exists
if [ ! -f "$SERVER_BIN" ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Server binary not found: $SERVER_BIN${NC}"
    echo "Please build the server first: make"
    exit 1
fi

# Function to check if server is running
is_running() {
    if [ -f "$PID_FILE" ]; then
        PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE")
        if ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            return 0
        else
            # PID file exists but process is dead
            rm -f "$PID_FILE"
            return 1
        fi
    fi
    return 1
}

# Function to start server
start_server() {
    if is_running; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Server is already running (PID: $(cat $PID_FILE))${NC}"
        return 1
    fi

    echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO] Starting Oblivion Server...${NC}"
    
    # Change to server directory
    cd "$SERVER_DIR"
    
    # Start server in background
    nohup "$SERVER_BIN" > "$LOG_FILE" 2>&1 &
    SERVER_PID=$!
    
    # Save PID
    echo $SERVER_PID > "$PID_FILE"
    
    # Wait a moment to check if it started successfully
    sleep 2
    
    if is_running; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}[SUCCESS] Server started successfully (PID: $SERVER_PID)${NC}"
        echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO] Log file: $LOG_FILE${NC}"
        return 0
    else
        echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Server failed to start${NC}"
        echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Check log file: $LOG_FILE${NC}"
        rm -f "$PID_FILE"
        return 1
    fi
}

# Function to stop server
stop_server() {
    if ! is_running; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Server is not running${NC}"
        return 1
    fi

    PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE")
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Stopping Oblivion Server (PID: $PID)...${NC}"
    
    # Try graceful shutdown first
    kill $PID 2>/dev/null
    
    # Wait for process to stop
    for i in {1..10}; do
        if ! ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            break
        fi
        sleep 1
    done
    
    # Force kill if still running
    if ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARNING] Server did not stop gracefully, forcing shutdown...${NC}"
        kill -9 $PID 2>/dev/null
        sleep 1
    fi
    
    # Clean up
    rm -f "$PID_FILE" "$LOCK_FILE"
    
    if ! ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}[SUCCESS] Server stopped${NC}"
        return 0
    else
        echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Failed to stop server${NC}"
        return 1
    fi
}

# Function to restart server
restart_server() {
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Restarting Oblivion Server...${NC}"
    stop_server
    sleep 2
    start_server
}

# Function to show server status
show_status() {
    echo "=================================================="
    echo "  OBLIVION SERVER STATUS"
    echo "=================================================="
    
    if is_running; then
        PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE")
        echo -e "Status: ${GREEN}RUNNING${NC}"
        echo "PID: $PID"
        echo "Binary: $SERVER_BIN"
        echo "Log file: $LOG_FILE"
        
        # Show process info
        echo ""
        echo "Process Info:"
        ps -p $PID -o pid,ppid,user,%cpu,%mem,etime,cmd | head -2
        
        # Show port listening
        echo ""
        echo "Port Status:"
        if command -v netstat > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            netstat -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep 54321 || echo "Port 54321 not found in netstat"
        elif command -v ss > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            ss -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep 54321 || echo "Port 54321 not found in ss"
        else
            echo "Cannot check port (netstat/ss not available)"
        fi
        
        # Show recent log entries
        if [ -f "$LOG_FILE" ]; then
            echo ""
            echo "Recent Log (last 5 lines):"
            tail -5 "$LOG_FILE" | sed 's/^/  /'
        fi
    else
        echo -e "Status: ${RED}STOPPED${NC}"
        if [ -f "$LOG_FILE" ]; then
            echo ""
            echo "Last Log Entry:"
            tail -1 "$LOG_FILE" | sed 's/^/  /'
        fi
    fi
    
    echo "=================================================="
}

# Function to show log
show_log() {
    if [ ! -f "$LOG_FILE" ]; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Log file does not exist yet${NC}"
        return 1
    fi
    
    if [ "$1" == "tail" ] || [ "$1" == "-f" ]; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO] Showing live log (Ctrl+C to exit)...${NC}"
        tail -f "$LOG_FILE"
    else
        # Show last 50 lines
        tail -50 "$LOG_FILE"
    fi
}

# Function to reload (restart) server
reload_server() {
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Reloading server configuration...${NC}"
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[NOTE] Server will be restarted to apply changes${NC}"
    restart_server
}

# Function to show help
show_help() {
    echo "Oblivion Server Management Script"
    echo ""
    echo "Usage: $0 { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }"
    echo ""
    echo "Commands:"
    echo "  start   - Start the server"
    echo "  stop    - Stop the server"
    echo "  restart - Restart the server"
    echo "  status  - Show server status"
    echo "  watch   - Watch server log in real-time (same as 'log tail')"
    echo "  log     - Show server log (use 'log tail' for live log)"
    echo "  help    - Show this help message"
    echo ""
}

# Function to watch log (alias for log tail)
watch_log() {
    show_log "tail"
}

# Interactive mode
interactive_mode() {
    show_help
    echo "Enter a valid option: "
    read -r option
    case "$option" in
        start)
            start_server
            ;;
        stop)
            stop_server
            ;;
        restart)
            restart_server
            ;;
        status)
            show_status
            ;;
        watch)
            watch_log
            ;;
        log)
            echo "Enter 'tail' for live log or press Enter for last 50 lines: "
            read -r log_option
            show_log "$log_option"
            ;;
        help)
            show_help
            ;;
        *)
            echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Invalid option: $option${NC}"
            echo "Enter a valid option: "
            read -r option2
            case "$option2" in
                start) start_server ;;
                stop) stop_server ;;
                restart) restart_server ;;
                status) show_status ;;
                watch) watch_log ;;
                log) show_log ;;
                help) show_help ;;
                *) echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Invalid option${NC}"; exit 1 ;;
            esac
            ;;
    esac
}

# Main script logic
if [ $# -eq 0 ]; then
    # No arguments provided, run in interactive mode
    interactive_mode
else
    # Arguments provided, run command directly
    case "$1" in
        start)
            start_server
            ;;
        stop)
            stop_server
            ;;
        restart)
            restart_server
            ;;
        status)
            show_status
            ;;
        watch)
            watch_log
            ;;
        log)
            show_log "$2"
            ;;
        reload)
            reload_server
            ;;
        help)
            show_help
            ;;
        *)
            echo -e "${RED}Usage: $0 { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }${NC}"
            echo "Enter a valid option: "
            read -r option
            case "$option" in
                start) start_server ;;
                stop) stop_server ;;
                restart) restart_server ;;
                status) show_status ;;
                watch) watch_log ;;
                log) show_log "$2" ;;
                help) show_help ;;
                *) echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Invalid option${NC}"; exit 1 ;;
            esac
            ;;
    esac
fi

exit $?

`server/oblivion-start`:

```
#!/bin/bash
# Oblivion Server Management Script
# Usage: ./oblivion-start { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }

# Configuration
SERVER_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
SERVER_BIN="$SERVER_DIR/oblivion-server"
PID_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/oblivion-server.pid"
LOG_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/server.log"
LOCK_FILE="$SERVER_DIR/oblivion-server.lock"

# Colors for output
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

# Check if server binary exists
if [ ! -f "$SERVER_BIN" ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Server binary not found: $SERVER_BIN${NC}"
    echo "Please build the server first: make"
    exit 1
fi

# Function to check if server is running
is_running() {
    if [ -f "$PID_FILE" ]; then
        PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE")
        if ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            return 0
        else
            # PID file exists but process is dead
            rm -f "$PID_FILE"
            return 1
        fi
    fi
    return 1
}

# Function to start server
start_server() {
    if is_running; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Server is already running (PID: $(cat $PID_FILE))${NC}"
        return 1
    fi

    echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO] Starting Oblivion Server...${NC}"
    
    # Change to server directory
    cd "$SERVER_DIR"
    
    # Start server in background
    nohup "$SERVER_BIN" > "$LOG_FILE" 2>&1 &
    SERVER_PID=$!
    
    # Save PID
    echo $SERVER_PID > "$PID_FILE"
    
    # Wait a moment to check if it started successfully
    sleep 2
    
    if is_running; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}[SUCCESS] Server started successfully (PID: $SERVER_PID)${NC}"
        echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO] Log file: $LOG_FILE${NC}"
        return 0
    else
        echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Server failed to start${NC}"
        echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Check log file: $LOG_FILE${NC}"
        rm -f "$PID_FILE"
        return 1
    fi
}

# Function to stop server
stop_server() {
    if ! is_running; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Server is not running${NC}"
        return 1
    fi

    PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE")
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Stopping Oblivion Server (PID: $PID)...${NC}"
    
    # Try graceful shutdown first
    kill $PID 2>/dev/null
    
    # Wait for process to stop
    for i in {1..10}; do
        if ! ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            break
        fi
        sleep 1
    done
    
    # Force kill if still running
    if ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARNING] Server did not stop gracefully, forcing shutdown...${NC}"
        kill -9 $PID 2>/dev/null
        sleep 1
    fi
    
    # Clean up
    rm -f "$PID_FILE" "$LOCK_FILE"
    
    if ! ps -p $PID > /dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}[SUCCESS] Server stopped${NC}"
        return 0
    else
        echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Failed to stop server${NC}"
        return 1
    fi
}

# Function to restart server
restart_server() {
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Restarting Oblivion Server...${NC}"
    stop_server
    sleep 2
    start_server
}

# Function to show server status
show_status() {
    echo "=================================================="
    echo "  OBLIVION SERVER STATUS"
    echo "=================================================="
    
    if is_running; then
        PID=$(cat "$PID_FILE")
        echo -e "Status: ${GREEN}RUNNING${NC}"
        echo "PID: $PID"
        echo "Binary: $SERVER_BIN"
        echo "Log file: $LOG_FILE"
        
        # Show process info
        echo ""
        echo "Process Info:"
        ps -p $PID -o pid,ppid,user,%cpu,%mem,etime,cmd | head -2
        
        # Show port listening
        echo ""
        echo "Port Status:"
        if command -v netstat > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            netstat -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep 54321 || echo "Port 54321 not found in netstat"
        elif command -v ss > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            ss -tulpn 2>/dev/null | grep 54321 || echo "Port 54321 not found in ss"
        else
            echo "Cannot check port (netstat/ss not available)"
        fi
        
        # Show recent log entries
        if [ -f "$LOG_FILE" ]; then
            echo ""
            echo "Recent Log (last 5 lines):"
            tail -5 "$LOG_FILE" | sed 's/^/  /'
        fi
    else
        echo -e "Status: ${RED}STOPPED${NC}"
        if [ -f "$LOG_FILE" ]; then
            echo ""
            echo "Last Log Entry:"
            tail -1 "$LOG_FILE" | sed 's/^/  /'
        fi
    fi
    
    echo "=================================================="
}

# Function to show log
show_log() {
    if [ ! -f "$LOG_FILE" ]; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Log file does not exist yet${NC}"
        return 1
    fi
    
    if [ "$1" == "tail" ] || [ "$1" == "-f" ]; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO] Showing live log (Ctrl+C to exit)...${NC}"
        tail -f "$LOG_FILE"
    else
        # Show last 50 lines
        tail -50 "$LOG_FILE"
    fi
}

# Function to reload (restart) server
reload_server() {
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[INFO] Reloading server configuration...${NC}"
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[NOTE] Server will be restarted to apply changes${NC}"
    restart_server
}

# Function to show help
show_help() {
    echo "Oblivion Server Management Script"
    echo ""
    echo "Usage: $0 { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }"
    echo ""
    echo "Commands:"
    echo "  start   - Start the server"
    echo "  stop    - Stop the server"
    echo "  restart - Restart the server"
    echo "  status  - Show server status"
    echo "  watch   - Watch server log in real-time (same as 'log tail')"
    echo "  log     - Show server log (use 'log tail' for live log)"
    echo "  help    - Show this help message"
    echo ""
}

# Function to watch log (alias for log tail)
watch_log() {
    show_log "tail"
}

# Interactive mode
interactive_mode() {
    echo "Usage: $0 { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }"
    echo "Enter a valid option: "
    read -r option
    case "$option" in
        start)
            start_server
            ;;
        stop)
            stop_server
            ;;
        restart)
            restart_server
            ;;
        status)
            show_status
            ;;
        watch)
            watch_log
            ;;
        log)
            echo "Enter 'tail' for live log or press Enter for last 50 lines: "
            read -r log_option
            show_log "$log_option"
            ;;
        help)
            show_help
            ;;
        *)
            echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Invalid option: $option${NC}"
            echo "Enter a valid option: "
            read -r option2
            case "$option2" in
                start) start_server ;;
                stop) stop_server ;;
                restart) restart_server ;;
                status) show_status ;;
                watch) watch_log ;;
                log) show_log ;;
                help) show_help ;;
                *) echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Invalid option${NC}"; exit 1 ;;
            esac
            ;;
    esac
}

# Main script logic
if [ $# -eq 0 ]; then
    # No arguments provided, run in interactive mode
    interactive_mode
else
    # Arguments provided, run command directly
    case "$1" in
        start)
            start_server
            ;;
        stop)
            stop_server
            ;;
        restart)
            restart_server
            ;;
        status)
            show_status
            ;;
        watch)
            watch_log
            ;;
        log)
            show_log "$2"
            ;;
        reload)
            reload_server
            ;;
        help)
            show_help
            ;;
        *)
            echo "Usage: $0 { start | stop | restart | status | watch | help | log }"
            echo "Enter a valid option: "
            read -r option
            case "$option" in
                start) start_server ;;
                stop) stop_server ;;
                restart) restart_server ;;
                status) show_status ;;
                watch) watch_log ;;
                log) show_log "$2" ;;
                help) show_help ;;
                *) echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] Invalid option${NC}"; exit 1 ;;
            esac
            ;;
    esac
fi

exit $?
